- Phase 77 (new-device auto restore): **proven only on a fake remote, fake IndexedDB, and one 360 px dev-server look at `/settings/backup/restore` (once showed the signed-out state, once "Page not found" because the boot sync with an unreachable test gym dropped the seeded policy; no overflow, no small targets, no page errors in either). (5) Item 5 [A-R4] is satisfied by not checking membership at all (the planner carries `memberStatus` and ignores it); the gym's own locks still apply to gym features. (6) Item 7 "closed or moved shard" = the member's `train_shard` is not in this app's config list; a shard that is in the config but down looks like an offline wait. (7) Item 12: the chunk restore does not use pull cursors, but cursors are reset on an empty phone so the delta pull that follows (`syncNow('manual')`) refetches everything and merges. (8) Item 10: the install gate shows one static line ("Your data returns after you sign in") on every step screen; it cannot know whether the person is a gym member because the gate makes no data call. (9) The "export, then start fresh" choice for a different owner only links to Data and backup (as in Phase 73). (10) Restore progress counts keys (parts), not months; the manifest has no month field beyond the key, so "n of m" is parts. - Phase 76-b1 (adapter + triggers): **the adapter never sent a real request** (fake `fetch` only: URLs, headers, bodies and answers are my reading of `contract/connected-api.md` 1.1.0; column list in `COLS`, the `owner_uid` / `member_id` fields of the insert, Storage routes `/storage/v1/object/...` and `/object/authenticated/...`, and the Storage "Duplicate" answer (400 with a 409 in the body, or 409) were NOT seen against a real Supabase). **Nothing starts any of it:** `startBackupTriggers`, `createBackupRemote` and `runBackupPass` are not called from app boot, so no backup happens in the running app yet (that is 76-b2); the caller must supply the shard token, `ownerUid`, shard id and the `enabled` check (mode not standalone AND feature `cloud_backup`; the feature resolver is not wired into the scheduler). **I edited an existing file, `src/data/local/repos.ts` (a change listener after put / remove), and did NOT re-run `tests/data/localStore.test.ts` or any suite that uses the repos** (rule: only this phase's tests); the new `backupTriggers` test exercises put and remove through it and passed. Not run: `tests/guard.test.ts`. The daily check marks every key dirty, which makes a full three-way check of every chunk once a day: its cost on a large history (each `buildChunk` rebuilds all chunks) is unmeasured. Foreground / `online` / open triggers were tested only through the scheduler, not as real DOM events (`browserEnv` is untested).

The manifest/Storage call shapes in `BackupRemote` follow `contract/connected-api.md` (1.1.0) but were never sent. **Not run:** the Phase 73-75 suites (`restore`, `chunks`, `chunksLocal`, `gymSync`) although `backupRun.ts` imports `applyChunk` / `buildChunk` (no edits were made to those files), `tests/guard.test.ts`. **Known limits of 76-a:** (1) `buildChunk` rebuilds EVERY chunk to produce one (as in Phase 75): fine for a pass, slow if called per key in a loop on a large history; measure in 76-b. (2) `RESTORE` / `PULL_MERGE` of a single key is handled inside `pushKey` (download, verify, `applyChunk`, record base) but there is NO whole-restore orchestration, no `backup:restore` writer, no resume yet. (3) The dirty-clear guard (`dirtyStamp`) lives in memory: after an app restart a key that was re-dirtied during a push is simply pushed once more (safe, one extra push). (4) `backup:last.error_code` is a plain code string; the plain-words messages (GYM_CONNECTION P74-4) are 76-b/UI work.

Column lists beyond `chunks_03` are mine [A-P75.1] and `made_at` is derived [A-P75.2]: **the Admin side must agree, or `ingest` and a second app version will read different bytes.** Item-level session fields come back via `extra` (tested for one session with a cardio set and a skipped item); not tested: custom-exercise refs, a chunk above 512 KB (`too_big` is thrown, the caller must handle it), `applyChunk` speed on a phone, `CompressionStream` missing (raw path tested only by `gzip:false`). Time columns keep seconds, exact ISO strings live in `extra`. Followed gym routines, prefs and photos are never backed up. `applyChunk` does not record `B` (Phase 76/77 does, after it resolves). Nothing calls the engine yet.

- Phase 73 (restore safety net): **nothing ran against a real Supabase, a real wipe, a real phone or iOS Safari.** Proven only on fake IndexedDB with a fake remote: `tests/shared/emptyCheck.test.ts` (23) and `tests/data/restore.test.ts` (9), typecheck once clean. **Not re-run although `syncNow` in `src/data/gym/link.ts` was edited: `tests/data/gymSync.test.ts` (18 + 30 in the Phase 67 file), `tests/data/gymUpload.test.ts`, `tests/guard.test.ts` (new TSX and strings unscanned; written with existing classes, no new CSS, no hex).** **The restore pulls ten personal tables with `select *` and the [A-P67.1] column guesses; none was ever sent to a real server**, and the inverse in `src/shared/restoreMap.ts` is mine (the Admin DATA_MODEL is not in this zip). **A session restored from rows has lost its item-level fields** (plan, status, supersets, dayName, `doneAt` only if the server kept it): every restored item is `done`; the full session comes back only with the chunk restore (Phases 75-77). The custom-exercise ref is recognised only when that exercise already exists on the phone, otherwise it becomes a library ref. **The empty check can wrongly block pushes:** the base is the live row count at the last good sync, so deleting more than 30% of your rows in one go between two syncs gives `block_push` until the Restore screen is run (a restore that finds nothing new also resets the base). No screen explains a block except the Restore row on `/gym` (it shows only while the last sync's verdict, kept in memory, is not `ok`, so it is gone after an app restart until the next sync; `/gym/restore` by address always works). **Browser check (one, 360 px, dev server, headless Chromium, `?bypass-gate`) covered only: standalone redirect to `/settings/gym`, and the connected-but-signed-out state of `/gym/restore` (sign-in prompt, no overflow, no target under 44 px, no console errors).** The offer, running, done, failed, different-owner and nothing-to-restore states were **never drawn in a browser** (the sandbox has no remote); they are covered only by the store tests. Edge cases of `RECOVERY_PLAN.md` section 6 NOT built here: automatic restore after sign-in (Phase 77), newest-month-first and a month-by-month "n of m" (the screen counts tables, 10 steps; months need chunks), the Wi-Fi line above 500 rows, the expired/frozen policy check [A-R4], the closed-shard message, the install-gate line, the "update the app" message, an "export then start fresh" flow (the different-owner screen only links to Data and backup). Resumability is at table level through the pull cursors (a killed run starts again; merging makes a repeat harmless); a sign-out in the middle was not tried. `restoreBase` and `owner` live in `meta`, which `exportAll` and a backup file do not carry, so a phone restored from a backup file has no stamp and no base until its next good sync. **Measurement:** the numbers in `RECOVERY_PLAN.md` 4.6 come from made-up seeded data run by `scripts/measure-chunks.mjs`, not from real members, and leave out the manifest, `sha256`, HTTP overhead and phone CPU time. `docs/RECOVERY_PLAN.md` was edited here and the Admin zip's copy is now OUT OF DATE (they must be identical).

- Production build never run (owner rule). First proof is the GitHub Actions build on the first tag, or the owner running `npm run build` locally. Until then `dist/`, `dist/404.html` from a real build, Tailwind processing and the base path are unverified.
- Deploy workflow and `vercel.json` never executed. Owner one-time setup: GitHub Pages source = GitHub Actions (see `GITHUB_UPDATE_PUSH.md` section 4).
- vitest 5.1.0 needs `--legacy-peer-deps` (see A11).
- Phase 2 CSS never compiled: `index.css` (`@import` chain + `@tailwind utilities`), the Tailwind theme-from-variables config (preflight off) and the `@font-face` urls are untested because no build/PostCSS run was allowed. First proof is the GitHub build or a local `npm run build`. Nothing renders styled until then.
- Contrast ratios (4.5:1 body, 3:1 large/icons) are NOT tested: DESIGN_TOKENS.md mentions a contrast helper in Phase 2, but the PHASES.md Phase 2 block does not list one, so it was not added. Owner: add it to a later phase (e.g. Phase 3) or tell me to do it as a fix-pack item.
- Guard limits: CSS named colours other than the forbidden-hue names (e.g. `red`) are not detected; `src/**/*.test.*` files and `tests/` are not scanned; hue check is HSL-based on hex/rgb()/hsl() only (other colour functions are banned outright).
- Fonts: latin subset only. No rupee sign (U+20B9) in either face, so it falls back to a system font; no Devanagari/Kannada (planned for later).
- Fonts and any rendering were not looked at in a browser (no phone, no browser check: this phase creates no pages).
- Phase 3 primitives were tested in happy-dom only (DOM structure, events, ARIA, store logic). happy-dom has no layout, so real wrapping, 44 px targets, 360 px fit, the 4:3 frame, sheet/dialog positioning, safe-area padding and all animations are UNVERIFIED until a browser run (Phase 4+ pages / Phase 64 overflow audit).
- Not covered by tests: sheet drag-to-dismiss (pointer drag), focus restore after close, real Android haptics (only the `navigator.vibrate(8)` call is checked), iOS behaviour, reduced-motion CSS.
- Known limits by design: Sheet/Dialog have no exit animation and no full focus trap (focus moves in and is restored; Tab can leave the dialog). `Select` is the native element. Stepper has +/- only (no tap-to-type, no hold-to-repeat). Toast offset above the tab bar is `--toast-offset`, set by the shell in Phase 4.
- Only 11 icons are inlined so far (barbell, caret-left, caret-right, check, image, info, magnifying-glass, minus, plus, warning, x). A phase that needs another icon adds its name to `scripts/build-icons.mjs`, runs `node scripts/build-icons.mjs`, and commits `src/ui/icons/generated.ts`.

- Phase 4 shell checked only in desktop Chromium emulating 360 px on the Vite dev server (Linux sandbox). NOT checked: real phone, iOS Safari safe-area/notch with the tab bar, production base path `/shield-fitness-user/` (GitHub Pages) and the `404.html` deep-link fallback, other widths (320/390/414/430), page-transition feel, keyboard tab order. The overflow audit across all widths is Phase 64.
- `tests/guard.test.ts` was NOT re-run in Phase 4 (Verify line did not name it); the new `shell.css`, `Shell.tsx` and pages were written to the rules (tokens only, no truncation, no hex) but are unscanned.
- Phase 3 default labels (`src/ui/components/labels.ts`, English constants) were NOT switched to `t('key')`: not named in the Phase 4 scope. Later phase or fix pack.
- Tab pages, `/exercise/:slug`, `/machine/:slug`, `/routine/:id`, `/muscle/:id` are placeholders ("Coming soon") that show the slug; real screens come in their own phases.

- Phase 5: the PHASES.md Verify line "build emits `sw.js` + manifest" was NOT run (owner rule). Unproven until the GitHub build: vite-plugin-pwa 2.0.0 + injectManifest config, `src/sw.ts` bundling, manifest and `version.json` output, `%BASE_URL%` in index.html, glob patterns, the SW registering and updating, offline slug routes, the update toast, `bypass-gate` absent from `dist/` (the test is skipped until `dist/` exists). If vite-plugin-pwa 2.0.0 fails, fall back to the latest 1.x (see DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md).
- Phase 5 real devices: no iOS/Android install, no real `beforeinstallprompt`, no real in-app browser tested. Gate checked only in desktop Chromium with spoofed user agents (desktop, iPhone Safari, iPhone Chrome, Android, Instagram) at 360 px (no overflow, targets >= 44 px, no console errors) and the `?bypass-gate` path. The iOS "animated" 3-step guide is static numbered steps (no animation). Copy-link was seen to fall back to its warning toast (headless denies the clipboard); the success path is untested.
- Typecheck was run twice in Phase 5 (second run after a small edit to `main.tsx`); no other test beyond `tests/pwa`.
- Icons are placeholders (gold "S" in a ring); no theme-color meta tag (a hex in `index.html` would fail the guard; the manifest carries the colour).
- `npm audit` reports 4 moderate issues (glob/minimatch through workbox-build); build-time only, not fixed.

- Phase 6 tested only with fake-indexeddb in Node. NOT tested: real IndexedDB in Chromium/Safari (iOS eviction, quota errors, blocked upgrades with two tabs open), a real `navigator.storage.persist()` / `estimate()` (only guarded; no test), large photo data URLs, import of a very large file. `requestPersist()` exists but is NOT yet called anywhere (SYNC_SPEC says "after install"; wiring it is a later phase, e.g. 62/63 or a fix pack). `tests/guard.test.ts` not re-run. Nothing uses the stores yet, so no UI reads or writes them.
- Phase 6 did not touch `contract/`; no copy to the Admin zip is needed.

Parse time, search time and memory are unmeasured (need a device). The SW changes the spec asks of Phase 11 (ignore `ds-detail-*` in the precache, detail runtime route, idle warm) are not made yet. Phase 7 touched nothing in `contract/`.
- Phase 8 (data and docs only, no code, no tests, no typecheck): the Verify line was met with a throwaway Python script (not in the repo): every CSV parses, every axis value is in the engines.md vocabulary, every `muscle_id` is in the 34-id taxonomy, every machine name and exercise slug resolves, counts below. **Not done:** the real compiler has not run the rules (Phase 10), so the tagged-row figure (1,413 of 4,752) comes from my own matcher written from the spec text; the compiler may differ at the edges. Rule precision was hand-checked on samples of the riskiest rules only (standing, lying, leaning, incline, flat, alternating, seated, wide, narrow, rope); 454 rows involve an `ambiguous` rule. `setup_muscle_shift.csv` was authored from general strength-training knowledge, **not checked against a literature source** (the `reason` column says "tends to", `confidence` is my own rating); it needs the physio review before it is trusted. Alias effects on search ranking are untested (search code starts in Phase 11/20). "With fallback" reach numbers in `REACH_GAPS.md` come from a throwaway script with my own reading of the fallback expansion (Phase 12 owns it). `machine_companions.csv` `evidence=data` was found by running my rules over the variation rows, not by hand. `contract/CHECKSUMS.txt` was regenerated here and re-verified with `sha256sum -c` (58 files OK), but the Admin zip has not been touched.
- Phase 9: the compiler was run once on the real data; the generated JSON was never loaded by app code or a browser (the loader is Phase 11). Only the compiler's own fixture tests exist (`tests/dataset/compile.test.ts`); `tests/guard.test.ts` was not re-run (the compiler script and generated JSON are outside what the guard scans anyway).
- Phase 9: typecheck ran twice (once after the tests were written, once after a one-line test fix), not once. `npm install` ran once for `papaparse`; `npm audit` was not looked at.
- Phase 9 measured sizes (gzip): precached files total 35.5 KB (budget 60), index 11.4 KB, largest detail chunk 8.2 KB (budget 12). The 11 detail chunks total 79.7 KB gz (380 KB raw), more than the 57 KB estimated in `DATASET_RUNTIME.md` section 8 (the draft shape left out `hint`, `cautions`, `pattern`). Still within every budget; the idle-warm download in [A43] is therefore about 80 KB, not 57 KB. Parse/search time unmeasured (needs a device).
- Phase 10 (compiler only, nothing reads the new files yet): the new JSON was never loaded by app code or a browser. Typecheck ran three times (a test-type error, its fix, and a final clean run). `tests/guard.test.ts` not re-run. Parse and search time unmeasured.
- Phase 10 tagging is keyword matching, not understanding: 1,413 of 4,752 variation rows get at least one tag (the rest are tempo, load and similar and stay plain text). 311 rows were tagged by `ambiguous` rules and are listed in `review/untagged_variations.csv` for the owner/physio. The 90% check is only on rows that mention a grip or handle word (396 of 413 = 95.9%); it excludes the 40 rows about hook/snatch/clean/front-squat grips and lifting straps, which Phase 8 left out of the vocabulary on purpose (they are in the review file as `out_of_vocabulary_grip_or_aid`).
- Phase 10 machine reach is precomputed with a simplified `applySetup` (one option at a time; more x1.25 cap 1, absent+more = 0.3, less x0.75; pattern rows plus `any`). Phase 13 owns the real `applySetup` and Phase 23 may recompute at runtime; a reach filter combining two options (for example bench + handle) is not precomputed. Reach = the highest single-exercise muscle weight (see [A59]).
- Phase 10 found 42 exercise/machine aliases in `search_aliases.csv` whose canonical text is not exactly an exercise or machine name (they still work as query expansion; they just add no alias tokens to a document). Not an error.
- Phase 11 (runtime only, no pages): tested in Node with a 5-exercise fixture dataset and fake-indexeddb, plus one smoke test that loads the real manifest and one real exercise through the Vite glob. NOT tested: a real browser (the glob/dynamic-import chunking in a production build, chunk names, lazy network fetches), a real device, search time (budget < 50 ms for 700 docs is unmeasured), memory, ranking quality on the real 634 exercises beyond the one `ab wheel` hit (fixtures for >= 20 real queries are Phase 20). `tests/guard.test.ts` was not re-run. `npm install` was needed (fresh sandbox) and ran once.
- Phase 11 service-worker work from `DATASET_RUNTIME.md` section 6 was NOT done (PHASES.md Phase 11 does not name it): no `globIgnores: ['**/ds-detail-*.js']`, no `chunkFileNames` (`ds-detail-*` / `ds-core-*`) in `vite.config.ts`, no detail runtime route, and nothing calls `warmDetailChunks()` yet. Effect: the existing Phase 5 glob `**/*.js` will precache every dataset chunk including the 11 detail chunks (about 80 KB gz extra on first install), so offline should still work, but this is unproven until a real build. Tell me if you want it as a fix-pack item or in a later phase.
- Phase 11 search covers name, machine name, primary muscle names and category only (A47). Aliases ("butterfly", "lat pd"), setup words and the "injury-safe" filter are not in it; they come with Phase 20 (aliases/setup) and the avoid-marks phases (injury-safe).
- Phase 12 (pure code only): tested in Node. Weight tests use 7 real rows copied into the test file plus a check that `fallbackRows` reproduces the compiler's rows for all 42 fallback exercises (reads the real generated files and the master CSV). The "absent muscle gets 0.3" and "cap at 1.0" cases use small synthetic bases (no real row hit them in the first scan). NOT tested: `applySetup` (Phase 13), anything in a browser, `tests/guard.test.ts` (not re-run). `t()` params and the new `validation.*` strings are not shown in any screen yet. Validator limits (weight 0-1000 kg, reps 0-1000, name 60 characters) are my defaults, not owner data.

They are NOT yet reproduced by app code: Phase 15 (scoring, list) and Phase 35 (alternatives) must turn them into fixtures, and any mismatch means the doc or the code is wrong. The reason score adjustments (P14.6), the slot choice for lists, the `Session.clientId = id` reading and the pool-order-beats-global-sort reading are my choices, not owner data; the physio/coach has not seen them. `docs/ROUTINE_BUILDER.md` and `docs/FLEXIBLE_SESSION.md` were read, not edited (Phase 28 owns them).

The `my-cable-curl` jitter value in `suggest_jitter.json` was produced by the same Python hash, so it only proves the two implementations agree (the three FNV vectors are the independent check). The exercise link keeps only the first chosen chip. Custom exercises are not in the lab (no reach data). Setup modes (for example Lat Pulldown grip modes) are shown as plain text, not as chips. Reach comes from our data and is not a statement of what a machine can do. `npm audit` not looked at.
- Phase 24 (data and docs only, no code, no tests, no typecheck, no `npm install`): the Verify line was met with two throwaway Python scripts (not in the repo): one generated the CSVs, a second independent one re-read them and checked that every file parses, every pattern has all 12 joints in both poses, angles are in range, every rule and map row names an existing pattern, every map slug is in the 634-row master list and appears once, every param key is allowed, and every preset machine name exists in `machines.csv`. **Not done:** (1) no coach or physio has seen any angle: the pose numbers, anchors, `arm_world` rule and param effects are my own authoring from general knowledge, not from a source; (2) the mapping is keyword matching on names, hand-checked only on the largest patterns (squat, lunge, curl, presses, push-up, hinge, row, crunch, rotation, deadlift, jump, carry, hip thrust), so smaller patterns and the 8 `ambiguous` and 6 `low` rules may draw a wrong-looking figure; (3) nothing was drawn: the angle conventions (for example side-view shoulder = angle from the line to the hip, front-view hip = sideways spread) have never been run through forward kinematics, so Phase 25 may find a pose that looks wrong and will need to adjust a row; (4) the pattern count is 54, not "about 30" (A-P24.1), because 85% needed them; (5) `equipment_presets.csv` copies the Phase 19 table, which is still the author's guess (A-P19.2); the app does not read the CSV yet. The `contract/` folder changed (see Next for the owner). `tests/guard.test.ts` not run (no code touched).

## Assumptions [A] (change only on the owner's word)
- [A-P74.1..6] Phase 74 spec assumptions are listed in `docs/GYM_CONNECTION.md` (end of file): backup keys default off until Phase 78; build variable names for free shards; routes under `/settings/backup`; two recovery prompts; `consent_version` 1; draft English wording.
- [A-P73.1] Owner stamp = `member:<MEMBER CODE upper-cased>` taken from the code saved at sign-in; written once, at the first `ok` sync or the first finished restore, never replaced by a different member and kept by Disconnect (that is what makes a second member on the phone visible). A free account would use `free:<id>` (Phase 78). Stored in `meta`, so a backup file does not carry it.
- [A-P73.2] The base is the count of live rows in the synced stores (everything except `prefs` and `photos`, deleted rows not counted), written after each `ok` sync and after a finished restore. A phone that never synced has base 0. S1 reading: `restore_offer` when local is 0 and (base above 0 or the server is known to hold data); `block_push` when local is under 70% of base (exactly 70% is allowed); a brand-new member with nothing anywhere is `ok`.
- [A-P73.3] While signed in and the phone holds no live rows, each sync run asks the server once for one `workout_sessions` row to know whether it holds data. An error or offline is "unknown" and counts as not holding data.
- [A-P73.4] The restore refills from the EXISTING delta pull (`pullTable`, `PULL_LIMIT` pages, the cursor moves after the rows are stored) over ten tables with `select *`; before it starts, when the phone is empty, the cursors of those ten tables are dropped. Rows merge by `client_id`: newer `updated_at` wins, a tie keeps the phone's row, a local tombstone is never revived, nothing local is deleted. Sets and cardio rows are placed into their session (idempotent by item order + set index); a deleted or unknown session gets none. Writes go straight to the stores, never to the outbox, and the outbox switch is turned off for the run and put back as it was.
- [A-P73.5] The `restore_*` fixture lives in `tests/fixtures/restore_empty_check.json`, not in `contract/` (Phase 73 may not change it). It was worked by hand from `RECOVERY_PLAN.md` 4.5, so it proves doc and code agree.
- [A-P73.6] Route `/gym/restore` has no feature key (`contract/` is read-only, as A-P69.5). The Restore screen never runs while the forced password change is open or while signed out (it shows the sign-in prompt).
- [A-P73.7] A different member's phone (`different_owner`) only blocks pushes and says so; it never merges and offers a link to Data and backup. The full "export, then start fresh" flow is Phase 77.
- [A-P49.1] The time of an exercise for the recovery clock is the latest non-warm-up set's `doneAt` in that item, else the session's `startedAt`, else 0. Warm-up sets never move the clock. An item with no working set adds nothing. Open sessions count (their sets are real work).
- [A-P49.2] `readRecoveryStates()` returns `{}` when nothing was ever logged (and when the clock cannot be read), so rest-day hints stay hidden and floating mode keeps its rotation; with any logged work every muscle gets a state (never trained = `fresh`). The hint shows at >= 60% fatigued target muscles (the Phase 32 rule, unchanged) and only when `rest_suggestions` is on.
- [A-P49.3] The screens list back, abs, glutes, quads, calves and every leaf; the three parents drawn only through their children (chest, shoulders, traps; `regions: none` in the taxonomy) are left out of lists and counts but still colour the figure. The list is the constant `GROUP_ONLY` in `recoveryModel.ts`. Ready today = listed muscles that are `ready` or `fresh`.
- [A-P49.4] Marks: tapping the mark a muscle already has clears it; Clear is offered only when the muscle has its own mark; a mark inherited from a core parent is shown as a note and does not offer Clear (clear the parent). Marks screen has the text twin (`MuscleChips`) because figure regions can be tiny. Feature keys: `recovery_clock` for `/body/recovery`, `injury_marks` for `/body/marks` (no new key; balance has none, so it follows `recovery_clock`). Balance wording names the smaller side first ("Pull is 40% of your push work"); 28 days, 6-set floor and 75% rule are [A-P48.3].
- [A-P47.1] Sex and the "skipped" flag, the stored peaks and the confirmed sets live in one `prefs` row (`badges.profile`); bodyweight is one `bodyStats` row per IST day (`bw:<day>`, fields `day`, `kg`), typing again on the same day replaces it. Bodyweight is accepted from 25 to 400 kg. Both are local and ride the existing outbox like other rows.
- [A-P47.2] "Skip" on the first-run prompt sets `skipped`: the page shows only a "Badges are hidden" card with a Set up button, and no promotion banner appears. The Settings switch "Show muscle badges" flips the same flag. Sex is never defaulted.
- [A-P47.3] **Engine gap found, worked around in the repo, engine untouched:** `groupsOf` expects `parent_group` values, but the taxonomy has 18 of them; `abs`, `obliques`, `lower_back` (core) and `lats`, `traps` (back) would be dropped, leaving the Core group empty and Back without lats. `src/data/repos/badges.ts` folds those five parents into `core` / `back` before calling `groupsOf`. A later phase 46 fix should move this into `groupsOf` and add a fixture.
- [A-P47.4] Implausible sets (engine `needsConfirm`) are left out; one button on the page confirms all of them at once ("They are right, count them"), stored as `<sessionId>:<itemIndex>:<setIndex>`. There is no per-set review screen and no way to un-confirm.
- [A-P47.5] The promotion banner shows on the session summary only (not on History re-opens), reuses the record-burst box (`sf-sm__burst`, still under reduced motion), needs `pr_celebration` and `muscle_badges` both on, lists overall, then groups, then muscles, at most 6 lines. It compares the sessions before this one with the sessions up to and including it, at the session's own day.
- [A-P47.6] The "Strongest / weakest" card names the highest and lowest ranked group and only appears when at least two ranked groups differ in tier; its button opens `/heatmap` (where the weak-spot finder lives). The per-muscle sheet button "Train this muscle" opens `/muscle/<id>`.
- [A-P47.7] Tier crests are one shield SVG (a laurel arc added at Olympian), coloured by the existing `--tier-*` tokens; the tier name is written beside every crest and swatch. The body figure uses the same tokens through a new optional `tier` prop on `BodyPicker`; the muscle list under it is the text twin. Unranked muscles stay un-coloured.
- [A-P46.1] Unspecified sex averages `anchor_m` / `anchor_f` and also the two reference bodyweights (80 and 60 -> 70 kg) before the allometric formula. Thresholds use the latest body stat at or before `asOf`; a bodyweight lift (pull-up, dip, push-up) uses the body stat at or before its own session day; a day before the first stat uses the first stat; sessions after `asOf` are ignored. [A-P46.2] The strength window is 365 days: the `asOf` day and the 364 before it (a lift 365 days back is out). Experience, rust and peak are never windowed. [A-P46.3] Peak = max(stored peak, the tier of the all-time best lifts at today's thresholds, the current tier); a group peak = max(stored `group:<id>`, median of its muscle peaks, current tier). [A-P46.4] A set needs whole reps 1 to 12 (no rounding: Epley unrounded, reps 1 = the load), a weight above 0 (bodyweight lifts may have none); warm-up sets are ignored, drop and superset sets count. [A-P46.5] Group and overall tier = median of whole tiers, an even count averages the two middle ones and rounds down; a muscle ranked only through a 0.3 to 0.6 ladder drops one tier with a floor at Wood and still counts as ranked. Ties between ladders: the one with the higher rank weight (list order). [A-P46.6] Experience counts per muscle (non-warm-up, reps >= 3, muscle weight per set, 12 per muscle per session); group and overall experience = the median of the muscle experience tiers (no overall sum is invented). [A-P46.7] Rust = days since the last non-warm-up set of an exercise giving the muscle weight >= 0.3; more than 42 is rusty; never trained is not rusty. [A-P46.8] A promotion from Unranked counts only when the new tier is Beginner or higher (a first Wood is not a celebration); `sessionPromotions` compares the history before the session with the history including it, both at the session's day and without stored peaks. [A-P46.9] An implausible set (benchmark above 4.0 x the bodyweight of its day) is excluded and listed in `needsConfirm`; the caller marks it `confirmed: true` once the person says it is right. Sex `null` also returns `needs_input` (`needsSex`): the spec only names bodyweight, but nothing is assumed. No new route, string, CSS, icon or dependency.
- [A-P44.1] Weak spots: a group = a core muscle id (parent = itself) except `full_body` and `cardio`; an entry counts `sets x` the MAX weight of the group's muscles in that entry (the same max rule as the parent roll-up). The session count is finished sessions whose IST start day is inside the 28 days. Marks (avoid / sore) skip a group when the marked id is the group or belongs to it. [A-P44.2] Heat counts every session that has logged working sets, including one still open; the weak-spot session count uses finished sessions only. [A-P44.3] Period chips: Day / Week / Month start at today's period, the arrows step by one, Next is off once the period holds today; Custom opens a sheet (Last 7 / 14 / 30 / 90 days or two dates); All = first logged day .. today with no arrows. Compare = the period before (same kind; custom = same length); not offered for All. [A-P44.4] Compare tint: the band is the size of the change on the same 0.2 steps (`bandOf(abs(delta))`), gold ramp for more, steel ramp for less, never red; `BodyPicker` got an optional `loss` prop for it (a small edit of the Phase 17 component). [A-P44.5] Neglected strip: detailed muscles (not a parent that has children, no system ids) with no work in the period, never-trained first, then longest ago first, ties by id; the first 8 are shown with a Show all button. [A-P44.6] Tap sheet: band, sets that touched the muscle, up to 6 exercises (with the variation text) by contribution, last trained day (up to today), the parts of a parent id, and Train this -> `/muscle/<id>`. [A-P44.7] Add to my routine (weak spot): the active routine (else the first live one); the day is chosen by `pickDay` (first day that targets the group, else the lightest day); the exercise goes at the end of that day; a day that already has it says so and changes nothing; with no routine the buttons are off and a line says to make one. [A-P44.8] No new feature key (`heatmap` and `weak_spots` already exist), no new dependency, route `/heatmap` registered under the Body tab.
- [A-P43.1] The weekly p90 uses only the IST weeks (Monday start) in which that muscle was trained (raw > 0), over the person's whole history; weeks with zero work are not counted. Percentile = nearest rank, sorted ascending, index ceil(0.9 n) - 1. A muscle with no history uses the floor 6. [A-P43.2] "All time" is treated like a custom range: ref = base x days / 7, where days = first logged day .. today (a future-dated entry extends it). A one-day custom range uses base / 7 (a Day period uses base / 3). [A-P43.3] Set units: reps row 1; a row with time and no reps = seconds / 300 (a 60 s plank = 0.2); a row with both counts as a reps row; warm-up 0; a row with neither 0. [A-P43.4] heat01 is rounded to 9 places (so 1.2 / 6 is exactly 0.2); a value exactly on a band edge goes to the higher band; a zero reference with work done counts as full. Parent regions show the MAX heat01 of the muscle and its children (core ids named by a custom exercise count as the parent's own value). Compare delta = current - base, a muscle untrained in both is left out. No new feature key, route, string or dependency.
- [A-P40.1] No new route and no new feature key: the three buttons use `ai_review_export`. The session button sits on the page shared by the summary and every history entry (`SessionViewPage`), so one button covers both. [A-P40.2] Review a period lives on History; the exercise trend button sits in the exercise page's History block. [A-P40.3] Exercise trend: the list shows the last N (default 8) sessions with working sets; the first / best / latest summary line covers ALL logged sessions of that exercise. [A-P40.4] Skipped items print `Skipped (reason)`; items with no sets that are not skipped are left out; swaps are not linked. [A-P40.5] Name, bodyweight and body stats are typed in the sheet (no stored source yet), off every time it opens, and never saved; the saved options are intro, units and notes / RPE / setups / PRs / muscles. [A-P40.6] Editing the text is kept until any option changes, then the text is rebuilt (the sheet says so). [A-P40.7] When the Clipboard API fails the text area is selected, a legacy copy is tried, and the sheet tells the person to copy by hand if that fails too. [A-P40.8] Missing weight or reps print `-` (a bodyweight set reads `- x 10`), per engines.md 18. [A-P40.9] A range uses finished sessions whose IST start day is inside from..to; more than 30 sessions drops the per-session lines.
- [A-P39.1] No new feature keys (that would change `contract/`): summary, history and detail use `flexible_session`; the record burst and the PR wall use `pr_celebration`; the board stays on `pr_board`. [A-P39.2] The PR board already exists from Phase 37 at `/progress/records`; Phase 39 adds a Board / Wall switch there instead of a second `/records` page. [A-P39.3] A session belongs to the IST day it started on. [A-P39.4] Calendar is Monday-first, one dot per day with workouts; tapping a day filters the list. [A-P39.5] The burst shows only on the summary right after finishing, not when History opens the same screen later. [A-P39.6] Mini body shows muscles of exercises with at least one working set (primary beats secondary), with a Front/Back switch.
- [A-P38.1] SetLog got four optional local fields (`distanceM`, `incline`, `calories`, `restSec`) for cardio rows and the real rest; the contract SetLog shape is unchanged. [A-P38.2] Overload mode / intensity default to double / moderate until Settings. [A-P38.3] Smart rest timer on: class defaults + learning (engines.md 20); off: the routine's `restSec`, else the class default. No timer after warm-up sets. [A-P38.4] Plate calculator works in kg; bar and rack saved on the device (`logger:prefs`). [A-P38.5] Logger shows for open items only (todo / doing).
- [A1] Dependency versions were looked up on npm on 2026-11-16 and NOT install-tested; Phase 1 verifies and logs them.
- [A2] Supabase free-tier limits are unverified; re-check before relying on them.
- [A3] BodyParts3D licence (CC BY 4.0 vs legacy BY-SA) must be confirmed by the owner before a commercial launch.
- [A5] Open Food Facts (public API, ODbL) is the barcode source; user-triggered network call.
- [A6] Rest-day hints are on by default and never block training.
- [A7] Plan-share limits (10 creates / 60 claims per IP per 10 min, 48 KB, 300 live rows) are assumptions, tunable by env on the relay.
- [A8] Movement patterns are approximate typical ranges; plate calculator defaults, warm-up ramp percentages, rest defaults and streak grace rule are assumptions (editable).
- [A9] Muscle-badge tier fractions, reference bodyweights (80 kg M / 60 kg F), bodyweight fractions for pull-up/dip/push-up and every non-published ladder anchor are assumptions pending sport-scientist review. Published anchors: squat/bench/deadlift 90th percentile (van den Hoek et al. 2024, read via a secondary summary; primary paper not yet checked). Allometric exponent 0.67 per NSCA guidance.
- [A4] Connection phases 65-70 wait until Admin Phase 9 has frozen `contract/` and the owner has copied it here.

- Phase 18 gaps: touch feel on a real phone/Safari not tested (headless Chromium at 360 px only; taps were mouse-style clicks). Nothing sets favourites, marks, routines or custom exercises yet, so the browser check saw an empty person: **why chips, the "Built for you" section and avoid/sore hiding were proven only by the fixture unit tests, never on screen**. Reasons `recovered`, `neglected`, `balances_week`, `progress_ready`, `same_variation` are never produced because the lab passes no session history (daysRested/weekSets/etc. need the session store; later phase). `tests/guard.test.ts` not re-run: the new TSX and CSS follow the token rules but are unscanned. `tests/pages/lab.test.ts` is not type-checked (typecheck ran before it was edited). The "Machine unavailable" section is wired but never filled (the lab does not pass `showAll`). Hide list is empty (no hide feature yet). Level and goal (track) are not passed to scoring (no profile pref yet). Equipment filter is per machine (one machine or "no machine"); the equipment *presets* (bodyweight/dumbbells/home/gym) are Phase 19. `/body` has no `Recovery`, heatmap or marks screens yet (later phases).

- Phase 19 gaps: touch feel on a real phone/Safari not tested (headless Chromium at 360 px only, mouse-style clicks). Not seen on screen: the sub-category chip row (needs a category with 2+ types selected; only the pure counts are tested), the recent-searches chips and their remove/clear buttons (never looked at in the browser; the saving to the device store on blur/Enter is also unobserved, only the pure `addRecent`/`removeRecent` are tested), the "Show 30 more" button, the status badges (maintenance / out of order) on machine cards, custom exercises in the list (none exist yet), keyboard use of the Filters sheet. Library search is the Phase 11 search (name, machine, muscle, category); aliases, "did you mean" and setup chips are Phase 20, so "lat pd" or "butterfly" finds nothing yet. Equipment mode filters the **library only**; suggestions, day builder and swaps (named in the added scope) do not exist yet or were not wired (the Muscle Lab from Phase 18 does not read the preset). Library has no muscle, goal (track) or injury-safe filter yet (Phase 18's lab has the first two; injury-safe needs the marks phase). `Input` (Phase 3) is typed without `type`, `placeholder`, `value`, `autocomplete`, so `LibraryPage` passes them through a spread; a later fix pack should widen `InputProps`. `tests/guard.test.ts` not re-run: the new TSX and CSS follow the token rules but are unscanned. The dev-only `?bypass-gate` flag is dropped from the URL when the library rewrites its query (dev convenience only; installed use is unaffected). Contrast, other widths (320/390/414), production base path and the offline behaviour of `/library` are unverified.

### Phase 69 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P69.1] The four program tables' column names are guesses (`programs(id,slug,name,days_per_week,weeks,goal,status,deleted_at)`, `program_days(id,program_id,position,name,target_muscles)`, `program_items(id,day_id,position,exercise_ref,sets,rep_min,rep_max,rest_sec,note)`, `member_programs(program_id)`); `parsePrograms` is the one place to change.
- [A-P69.2] The proposal row is `{client_id, slug, name, definition, status:'proposed'}` into `custom_exercises_shared`; the Admin side must agree. Proposing is not undone by editing or deleting the exercise afterwards.
- [A-P69.3] Disconnect does not ask "Keep a copy?" for each followed program (spec section 4): every one silently becomes an editable copy, so nothing is lost. Cheaper than a new question screen; say if you want the question.
- [A-P69.4] Upload state is kept per gym host (`gym:upload`), so a different gym asks again. "Uploaded" means queued into the outbox and saved as such; failures stay in the outbox's failed list (the Sync screen).
- [A-P69.5] No feature key exists for proposals or the history upload (`contract/` is read-only here): Propose to gym needs `custom_exercises` on and a signed-in connection; S10 needs only the connection.
- [A-P69.6] Program exercise refs all become library refs `{kind:'lib',slug}`; a `gym-...` or `my-...` ref has no local definition until a later pull of `custom_exercises_approved` (not built).

### Phase 68 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P68.1] "Expiring" = 7 days left or fewer; the last covered day counts as 0 days left.
- [A-P68.2] **No spec for the QR crc exists in this zip.** I chose CRC-16/CCITT-FALSE over the text `SF1:<CODE>` (code upper-cased), written as 4 upper-case hex digits. **Admin must read the same format** (owner: tell the Admin session, or tell me the Admin rule and I change `crc16` / `encodeMemberQr`).
- [A-P68.3] Request statuses: `queued` (phone only), `open`, `in_progress`, `done`, `rejected`; the real server names are unconfirmed (Admin `DATA_MODEL` not in the zip).
- [A-P68.4] A freeze request carries whole days 1-90; a complaint and an equipment report need a note (3 to 300 characters); one open request per kind.
- [A-P68.5] Every column name in the six reads (see `API_CONTRACTS.md`, Phase 68) and in the request insert (`kind`, `note`, `freeze_days`) is a guess, like [A-P67.1]; `src/data/gym/gymData.ts` (`pullGymData`) and `requestRow` in `src/shared/gymData.ts` are the places to change.
- [A-P68.6] Requests are queued in a `prefs` row, not the IndexedDB outbox (a new store would need a migration and a backup change; the phase did not name one). They go out on the next sync, idempotent by `client_id`.
- [A-P68.7] Attendance days come from `checked_in_at` turned into an IST day; the streak is not broken until the day ends (yesterday counts when today has no visit yet).
- 2026-11-21 | Phase 67 DONE (E, no version bump, `contract/` untouched). **Connect:** the confirm screen's Connect runs S7 (reach the gym, read `gym_settings` policy and contract_version with the anon key, check the major), and stores nothing unless all pass; then the policy is cached (`acceptPolicy`), the connection is saved in the `prefs` row `gym:connection`, the outbox is switched on, and the app goes to Sign in. **Sign in:** `lookup-login` -> Supabase email + password on the core shard, then the member's train shard (`members.train_shard`) with the same credentials; the password lives only in that call. A wrong password, unknown code and anything else give one sentence; `SFE004` shows a countdown; an unknown `SFE` code shows the server's message. A forced change (`profiles.must_change_password`) sends the member to the Change password screen, which is the only gym screen until done (a Disconnect button is the one way out). **Sync:** outbox push in order (parents first), batches of 50, `upsert on_conflict` (ignore-duplicates for append-only tables), back-off `min(2^n x 5 s, 15 min)` + jitter, a rejected batch is retried row by row so one bad row cannot hold the rest, rejected rows become `failed` with a plain reason and stay visible, a 401 is retried once then the queue pauses ("sign in again"); one loop at a time; delta pull with a `(updated_at, id)` cursor kept as the server's string and moved only after storing; triggers: app open, foreground (30 s debounce), after sign-in / password change, online event, manual. **UI:** sync chip (Today and Gym home; hidden standalone), `/settings/gym/sync` (Pending screen: waiting count, failed items with reasons, Retry, Sync now), connected Gym home (name, host, policy version, signed in or not, Refresh, Sync, Disconnect). **Disconnect:** forgets config, session, policy and cursors, empties the outbox after remembering which rows were waiting (`gym:unsynced`, for Phase 69), keeps every personal row. Tests run: typecheck (clean, see gaps for the count), `tests/shared/sync.test.ts` + `tests/data/gymSync.test.ts` (30 pass), one browser check at 360 px on the dev server. Standing gaps above.
### Phase 67 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P67.1] Server column names are unknown here; `toServerOps` in `src/shared/sync.ts` is the one place to change them (see the gap).
- [A-P67.2] The Admin "9-18 deployed" condition cannot be read from this zip; the phase was done against a fake remote as its Verify line says. Nothing real was contacted.
- [A-P67.3] Screens import the gym link through the facade `src/data/repos/gymLink.ts` (rule: pages never import `src/data/gym`). `SyncChip` is on Today, so that facade is in the Today chunk (small, no Supabase).
- [A-P67.4] S13 (Join this gym) and the "account paused" sentence are left to Phase 68, S10/S11 to Phase 69.
- [A-P67.5] A contract-major mismatch found later (the `contract_version` row changes) sets `versionMismatch`: the outbox is switched off, the cached policy is cleared, the chip says "Needs attention"; it clears itself when the row matches again (the policy then comes back with the next pull).
- [A-P67.6] Disconnect does not mark rows `unsynced` one by one; it stores the list of waiting rows in the `prefs` row `gym:unsynced` (same effect, no write to every personal row).
- [A-P67.7] Supabase sessions are stored by the client under the keys `sf-gym-<host>-<shard>` in this origin's storage (refresh token only). Disconnect signs them out locally.

### Phase 66 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P66.1] The Scan and Paste screens are two modes of one page `/settings/gym/add` instead of separate routes (spec S3, S4); same behaviour, fewer routes.
- [A-P66.2] Fixtures were added under `contract/fixtures/` (same names for the Admin zip), a patch-level change: `VERSION` stays 1.0.0.
- [A-P66.3] `resolveFeatureValue` with no user value returns the admin `enabled` for an unlocked key, so the starting default is live until the user sets their own switch; the Settings screens still read the user's own pref as before.
- [A-P66.4] A policy with the same `version` as the cached one is accepted (idempotent refresh); only a lower one is stale.
- [A-P66.5] The member's own `my-...` exercises are never hidden by the gym; YouTube overrides must be `https` on youtube.com, www.youtube.com or youtu.be, anything else is ignored.
- [A-P66.6] Newer-format publishable keys (`sb_publishable_...`) are accepted as public keys; `sb_secret_...` and any JWT whose role is not `anon` are rejected with `GCF_SECRET`.
