### Phase 63 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P63.1] The six new pages are `MORE_GROUPS` in `src/shared/settings.ts`, separate from the six pref groups, so the Phase 62 tests and the "no orphan pref" rule still hold. Privacy already existed (Phase 42, plan sharing); I added three sections (what is stored, photos stay on device, the internet) and moved its Back arrow to `/settings`.
- [A-P63.2] Backup file never contains progress photos (a photo is a data URL and the Privacy page promises they never leave the phone). Restore merges by `client_id`, newer wins, nothing on the phone is removed (existing `importAll`). Wipe deletes the whole on-device database after typing `DELETE`, then reloads at `/`.
- [A-P63.3] Reminders: saved as one `reminders:prefs` row (`on`, `days` 0=Mon..6, `time` HH:MM IST; default off, Mon/Wed/Fri 18:00). **Nothing fires.** A web app cannot be woken by a schedule without a push server; the page says so and shows the next moment it would point at. Firing while the app is open is a later decision.
- [A-P63.4] Gym connection page is static text plus a disabled button until Phase 66.
- [A-P63.5] About fixed facts (name, what the app is, version from `package.json`, build id) are code and strings; only tagline, paragraphs, contact and links come from `about.json`. Credits: `thirdParty` is pre-filled (the seven named licences plus the runtime libraries); if `credits.json` is damaged or its list is empty the page falls back to `REQUIRED_THIRD_PARTY`, so it can never render empty. The "generated from package.json by a script" idea in SETTINGS_MAP was NOT built (the phase did not name a script); the runtime list is written by hand.
- [A-P63.6] "Owner to fill" is a visible dashed-gold box with one calm line, not an error.

### Phase 62 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P62.1] Six groups only (the ones the phase names): Account, Training, Workout flow, Body and goals, Appearance, Simple or Advanced. Data, Privacy, Reminders, Gym connection, About, Credits are Phase 63. Each group is its own registered route (feature `null`, core screens, Me tab); the page reads its group from the last part of the address because the registry's lazy pages take no props.
- [A-P62.2] Prefs: one row `settings:<key>` per pref in the device `prefs` store (never synced), each with a default; a stored value that is wrong reads as the default. Two groups of prefs live as a field of a row another screen already reads, so both sides agree: Overload mode and Intensity -> `overload:prefs` (`mode`, `intensity`), Simple / Advanced -> `logger:prefs` (`mode`). Writing one keeps the row's other fields (plates, bar weight).
- [A-P62.3] Account: name (up to 30), birth year (empty or 1900..2100), fitness level (Not set / Beginner / Intermediate / Advanced). Sex, height and weight were NOT duplicated: they already live in the badges profile and Body stats, so two link rows open `/profile/muscles` and `/progress/body` (shown only when those features are on). Defaults: units kg, rest 90 s (15 to 600, steps of 15), smart rest on, overload Double, intensity Moderate, the five flow switches on, heatmap period Week, recovery clock on, motion Full, haptics on, density Comfortable, mode Simple.
- [A-P62.4] "Set by your gym": a pref names its catalogue feature (`suggestions`, `warmup_suggestions`, `smart_rest_timer`, `overload_suggestions`, `intensity_dial`, `heatmap`, `recovery_clock`); when `lockedByGym` says so, the row stays visible, shows the current value as text and the words "Set by your gym", and the control is not drawn. The check is at read time only and never writes the stored pref. No resolver is installed, so nothing is locked until Phase 66.
- [A-P62.5] Theme is a fixed note ("Dark. A light theme comes later."), not a pref. The 3D low-power switch from SETTINGS_MAP is not built (the phase did not name it; Phase 60 keeps it per visit).
- [A-P62.6] Honest wording: pages that hold prefs no screen reads yet carry one line saying so; nothing claims an effect it does not have (see Standing gaps for the list).
- [A-P62.7] `setHapticsEnabled` was added to `haptics.ts` (and exported) so the Haptics switch has an effect; `base.css` got a `data-motion='reduced'` block that mirrors the system reduced-motion rules; `main.tsx` calls `loadAppearance()` once before the shell renders.

### Phase 61 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P61.1] "Longest streak" = the longest run of consecutive IST days with a finished session inside the range. The Progress ring's streak (Phase 53) counts weeks and has a grace week; the recap does not reuse it because a year card needs a stat over a past range, not "now". The card note says rest days are part of training.
- [A-P61.2] "Biggest PR" = the heaviest-weight record (metric `weight`) set inside the range with the largest gain over its earlier value, judged by the Phase 37 rule (`sessionPRs`) against ALL earlier finished sessions, also those before the range. A first-ever weight is a baseline, not a PR, so it is never the biggest. No card when there is none.
- [A-P61.3] "Least trained" is chosen only among muscles that got at least one primary working set in the range (a muscle with 0 sets would just be arbitrary), by primary-role sets from the dataset map; ties go to the muscle id alphabetically. Needs 2 or more muscles, otherwise the line is left out. The card says "least trained of the muscles you hit".
- [A-P61.4] Favourite exercise = done in the most sessions (ties: more working sets, then slug). Heaviest lift = largest single working-set weight (bodyweight sets have none).
- [A-P61.5] Ranges: This year, Last year, Last 90 days, Last 30 days, All time (first session to today), Pick dates (two native date inputs). An impossible or reversed range shows a message and reads nothing. Days are IST; a session counts on the IST day it started.
- [A-P61.6] Cards (in order, a card without data is left out): totals, heaviest, muscles, longest run, favourite, biggest PR. No sessions in the range = an empty state, no cards. Swipe = horizontal scroll-snap; Previous / Next buttons and "Card n of N" for anyone who does not swipe; reduced motion turns smooth scroll off. No shaming copy.
- [A-P61.7] Muscle of the week: ISO week of the IST day (Thursday rule); the unit is `rows[(isoYear * 53 + isoWeek) mod n]` over the atlas rows that have an "In daily life" line, in file order, so every week of a year picks a different muscle until all 39 are used, the same all week, no clock inside the pure function. The one fact is the first `inDailyLife` line. The card sits at the top of the Body tab and loads `atlas/logic` only when it opens (so the atlas text does not enter the Body chunk). It links to `/atlas` when `holo_atlas` is on. Not tied to the person's data.
- [A-P61.8] Routine image: layout is pure (`routineImage.ts`), drawing is `routineImageCanvas.ts`. Title (up to 2 lines), "N days", then the day blocks in one column (3 days or fewer) or two (4 to 7), each day name in gold and its exercise lines `name sets x reps` wrapped to the column. If it does not fit it first shrinks the text (to 55%), then shows fewer lines per day with a "+N more" line, and the sheet tells the person how many lines were cut. Footer is the app name. No photos, no name, no weights, no routine id. Colours and fonts are read from the CSS variables at draw time (no hex in code). Generated on the phone only (a test reads the three files and finds no network call, image load or URL).
- [A-P61.9] Share = the file through `navigator.share({files})` when `canShare` says yes, otherwise a PNG download; closing the share sheet does nothing. The file name is the routine name made safe.
- [A-P61.10] Entry points added (the phase named none): a "Year in review" link at the bottom of Progress, the card on the Body tab, and "Save as image" in the routine's options menu. All gated by their feature keys. The recap page is under the Me tab like the other progress pages.
- [A-P61.11] A routine image of a routine that holds a custom exercise shows its name (looked up like the routine page does) or "My exercise" if it cannot be found.

### Phase 60 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P60.1] Model size measured: `body.bin` 2,003,790 + `fit.bin` 625,563 + `meta.json` 14,581 = 2,643,934 bytes (about 2.6 MB). The "3.5 MB" in the docs was the prototype HTML (the same data as base64 plus code). The page says "about 2.6 MB". The two blobs are the prototype's gzip data byte for byte (the page unpacks them with `DecompressionStream`, so the file is named `.bin` to stop a host from adding its own gzip). Re-make with `node scripts/extract-atlas-model.mjs`.
- [A-P60.2] three.js is imported as ES modules (`three`, `three/examples/jsm/controls/OrbitControls.js`) in one file, `src/atlas/scene.ts`, loaded with `import()`, so it is a separate chunk (tested by reading the source: no other file imports three, the page reaches the scene only through `import()`, the route is `lazyPage`). The loose `three.min.js` file is not vendored; the npm package is the vendored copy (bundled, no CDN).
- [A-P60.3] Colours in the scene are read from the CSS variables (`--gold-400`, `--gold-200`, `--text`, `--bg-void`) at start, so no hex lives in code and there is no cyan. Selected unit = `--gold-200` glow with hatching off and a pulse; the rest dims. The prototype's "contested" amber, hover tooltip, Skin toggle, particles and group buttons are left out (not in the phase).
- [A-P60.4] A tap selects the whole unit (all heads, both sides). The prototype's rule that a tap through the external oblique can pick the rectus abdominis is kept.
- [A-P60.5] Panel = bottom `Sheet`: name, area, the approximate-shape note for the 5 fitted units, In daily life, "When it is weak" (the content describes weakness only, so the heading dropped "or tight"), Quick facts, See also chips, and the line "Educational information, not medical advice." No exercises, no Train link (`atlas.show_train_link` is not built; the phase did not ask).
- [A-P60.6] Fallback triggers: no WebGL, no `DecompressionStream`, a failed or non-OK fetch, no first frame in 20 s, a lost WebGL context, or the person pressing "Use the 2D figure" while loading. The 2D fallback shows the existing `BodyPicker` (male, pick-one) with the same list and sheet. A tap on a region opens the first unit listed for that id and offers the others as "Same area"; a parent id (chest, back, shoulders, traps) looks at its children only when it has no unit of its own (the taxonomy files hip flexors under abs, so using children for abs wrongly offered them; found by a test, fixed). Units with no drawn region show "Pick it from the list" text.
- [A-P60.7] Low power: automatic at 4 or fewer cores, 2 GB or less memory, data saver, or battery under 20% and not charging (battery only where the browser offers it); plus a switch on the page. It caps pixel ratio at 1, stops auto-rotate and flicker, and uses no antialiasing. Reduced motion stops auto-rotate and flicker too. The switch is not saved (no preference store yet).
- [A-P60.8] Rendering stops while the tab is hidden or the stage is off screen (visibility and IntersectionObserver), and the scene, geometries, materials and renderer are disposed when leaving the page. Data saver on = a screen asks before the 2.6 MB download; otherwise the model downloads on opening `/atlas` with a note and a "Use the 2D figure" button.
- [A-P60.9] Not precached: `public/atlas/` stays in the service worker's `globIgnores` and cached-on-demand rule from Phase 5 (checked by a test reading `vite.config.ts` and `src/sw.ts`; the behaviour itself needs the GitHub build).
- [A-P60.10] I added one link, "Body Holo Lab (3D)", on the Body tab beside Recovery and Avoid marks (gated by `holo_atlas`), because without an entry the page could not be reached. No other existing screen changed.
- [A-P60.11] Budget: no atlas budget number existed in the docs. I used the esbuild estimate only to report the size; no pass/fail threshold was invented. The chunk is lazy, so it does not touch the main bundle.

### Phase 59 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P59.1] `name`, `group`, `shape`, `meshKeys`, `taxonomyIds` were copied from `docs/atlas/atlas_units.csv` by a throwaway script (not kept, no new file); only the four text arrays and `seeAlso` were authored. The validator re-checks every copied field against the CSV, so the JSON cannot drift from it.
- [A-P59.2] The validator also rejects a small catch-list of exercise, equipment and brand words (squat, deadlift, sets, reps, dumbbell, barbell, kettlebell, machine, cable, workout, exercise, nike, youtube) to enforce the "no exercises" tone rule, plus links and emoji. The list is mine; it is not a full dictionary.
- [A-P59.3] The validator requires `reviewed === false` for every row, so a row cannot be marked reviewed by accident. Change that rule when sign-off starts.
- [A-P59.4] Order of rows = order of `atlas_units.csv` (checked). `seeAlso` links stay inside the atlas (all ids exist, none self, at most 3); no `seeAlso` is required to be symmetrical.
- [A-P59.5] No `package.json` script was added (the phase names none); run `node scripts/validate-atlas.mjs`.

### Phase 57 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P57.1] Servings use the card's per-serving protein (the source's own number, else per 100 g x serving grams / 100, as Phase 56 made it), times 0.5 / 1 / 2. Grams use per 100 g x grams / 100. The result is rounded once to 0.1 g. A product with no per-serving protein hides 0.5 / 1 / 2 and offers grams only.
- [A-P57.2] An unknown protein is refused with a message, never stored as 0 g. A result that rounds to 0 g is refused too (the intake log keeps only amounts above 0). One entry above 300 g (the intake limit) is refused as too much; grams typed above 5000 are refused as a bad amount. A comma decimal is accepted.
- [A-P57.3] The entry is the same `{ kind: 'protein', amount, day, at }` row the quick-add buttons write; the product name, barcode and other nutrients are not stored anywhere. The nutrition card itself is still never logged.
- [A-P57.4] Undo on the scan page removes the entry that was just added (by its id), not the newest entry of the day. It disappears after a new add, a portion change or leaving the page; the Undo on `/intake` still removes the day's newest entry.
- [A-P57.5] The protein ring is on `/intake`; the scan page shows the new day total in words and a link to `/intake`, so no second ring was added (no new route, no feature key, no icon; `barcode_scan` gates the page as before).
- [A-P57.6] Picker labels are 0.5 / 1 / 2 / Grams with the group label "Servings eaten, or grams", because longer labels split mid-word at 360 px in a four-way control.

### Phase 58 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P58.1] One text per **unit**, not per mesh: 74 mesh keys became 39 units (heads and parts of one muscle share a row). `PHASES.md` asked for "the meshes mapped to taxonomy ids"; every mesh and every taxonomy id still has an owner row, which is what Verify asks.
- [A-P58.2] The schema got four fields beyond the phase text, `shape`, `meshKeys`, `taxonomyIds` and `reviewed` (the last was already in `HOLO_ATLAS.md`). Phase 59 copies the first three from the CSV so the author writes only the text arrays and `seeAlso`.
- [A-P58.3] The "5 missing muscles" item became "5 fitted muscles" because of the swapped prototype (they are in 3D now). The 2D fallback covers them through lats, abs, obliques and lower_back, which have drawn 2D regions. Seven units have none (pectoralis minor, serratus anterior, rotator cuff, soleus, tibialis anterior, iliopsoas, sartorius) and are text-only there.
- [A-P58.4] The atlas `group` is the prototype's group (13), not the taxonomy parent; three disagreements are noted in the CSV (serratus anterior, teres major, tensor fasciae latae). Levator scapulae, coracobrachialis and fibularis have no taxonomy id of their own and are mapped to the nearest (upper_traps, biceps, calves).
- [A-P58.5] Limits not already in `HOLO_ATLAS.md` (name 40 chars, 700 chars of text per row, item counts, the banned-word list) are my choice; the 70 / 90 character limits were the owner's earlier ones.
- [A-P58.6] Atlas text is English in `atlas.json`; only the page labels use `t()`.

### Phase 56 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P56.1] A code read by the camera starts the look-up at once: the person's tap on Scan is the tap the spec asks for. Typed codes look up only on the Look up button. The look-up is never made on page load, on mount, from an effect, or from the cache read.
- [A-P56.2] Codes: digits only (spaces and hyphens ignored), 13 = EAN-13, 12 = UPC-A (looked up as the EAN-13 with a leading 0, how Open Food Facts stores it), 8 = EAN-8, or a UPC-E that expands to a valid UPC-A. The check digit must match; a mistyped number never reaches the network. GTIN-14 and other lengths are refused. While scanning, a Code-128 text that is not a valid code is ignored and scanning goes on; typed non-digits are refused with a message. Code-128 is requested from the detector as the spec says, but only a label carrying one of the digit codes above is accepted.
- [A-P56.3] Network first, cache second: the request is tried each time; only when it cannot be reached or the server errors is a saved copy of the same code shown, marked "Saved copy ... may be out of date". No saved copy = a plain `offline` / `server` message and nothing invented. The cache is one `prefs` row (`barcode:cache`), 50 products, newest first, one per code; prefs never reach the outbox. There is no screen listing the cache.
- [A-P56.4] Answer reading (my reading of the public v2 API, not checked against the live service): `status` 0 or HTTP 404 = not found; a product with no `product_name` (or `product_name_en`) = not found; a body that is not JSON or not an object, or `status` 1 without a product, = malformed. A number may be a number or a numeric string; negative or non-numeric = unknown (a dash, never 0). Per serving = the source's `*_serving` number, else the unrounded per-100 g number x serving grams / 100 rounded once (kcal whole, salt 2 dp, others 1 dp). Serving grams = `serving_quantity` when above 0, else the number before "g" in `serving_size`; millilitres do not count as grams. A product with no per-serving numbers hides the Per serving choice and says so. Salt is in grams.
- [A-P56.5] Request: GET `https://world.openfoodfacts.org/api/v2/product/<code>.json?fields=...` (only needed fields), no cookies, no referrer, 10 s timeout (a timeout counts as offline). The browser sends no custom User-Agent.
- [A-P56.6] The detector: native `BarcodeDetector` when it lists an EAN or UPC format, else the ponyfill. The ponyfill's WASM is imported with `?url` and handed to `setZXingModuleOverrides({ locateFile })`, because its default is a CDN fetch (breaks the no-CDN rule). `zxing-wasm@3.1.7` is therefore declared directly in `package.json`.
- [A-P56.7] No new route beyond `/intake/scan`, no new feature key (`barcode_scan` existed), no icon, no `contract/` change. The page has no tab of its own: it is reached from the Scan button on `/intake`.

### Phase 55 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P55.1] Protein target = latest logged body weight x factor (1.2 / 1.6 / 2 g per kg, default 1.6, chosen on `/intake`, device-only pref `intake-protein-factor`), rounded to the nearest 5 g; no weight in 25-400 kg = no target (the page says so and links to `/progress/body`). The spec named only "weight x factor"; the three factors are my reading.
- [A-P55.2] Water: one glass = 250 ml, daily target fixed at 2,000 ml (8 glasses); no setting yet.
- [A-P55.3] Generic typed `intake` rows `{ kind: 'protein' | 'water', amount (g | ml), day, at }`; `day` is the IST day of `at` when the row is made (so the IST roll-over is fixed at write time). Entries over 300 g or 5,000 ml are refused. Undo removes the newest entry of today (any kind). Rows queue to the outbox when connected (the contract names an `intake_logs` table); the shape is not frozen in `contract/`.
- [A-P55.4] Photos: `photos` store only (NEVER_SYNCED); the picked image is shrunk to a JPEG data URL, long side 1280 px, quality 0.82, before saving; at most 200 photos; compare = any two, earlier on the left; delete asks first. The warning card is always on the page. No camera capture attribute is set (the system picker offers camera or library).
- [A-P55.5] No menu entry: both pages are linked from `/progress` only.

### Phase 54 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P54.1] Body readings share the `bodyStats` rows the badges read: id `bw:<day>`, fields `day`, `kg`, plus optional `fat` (%) and `cm` {chest, waist, hips, arm, thigh}. One row per IST day; saving again keeps fields not typed. Ranges: weight 25-400 kg, fat 2-60 %, measurement 10-300 cm, height 100-250 cm.
- [A-P54.2] Height is a device-only pref (`body-height`); BMI = kg / m^2 (one decimal) from the latest weight, shown with a plain band (under 18.5, 25, 30) and a note that it cannot tell muscle from fat.
- [A-P54.3] Goals: kinds weight, body fat, lift (estimated 1RM of one exercise), attendance (sessions logged since the goal began), event (a named day). Start = latest reading (weight, fat), best e1RM (lift), 0 (attendance, event). Progress = share of the way from start to target in either direction, clamped 0-100 %; reached at or past the target. With a deadline: ahead / on track / behind compare progress with the share of time used (10 % tolerance); past the deadline and not reached = overdue. An event goal fills with time and is reached on its day.
- [A-P54.4] Milestones = 25 / 50 / 75 / 100 % marks between start and target (whole sessions for attendance; none for events). Achievement moment = the existing gold burst, shown once per goal (`achievedOn` is stored); reached goals move to a "Reached" list. It is not tied to the `pr_celebration` flag.
- [A-P54.5] Goals live in the `goals` store with fields `kind, target, start, startDay, deadline?, slug?, title?, achievedOn?`; the connected `goals` table shape is not frozen in `contract/`, so this is local until the sync phase.

### Phase 53 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P53.1] Weekly target = the person's own number (1 to 7, device-only pref `streak-target`, chips on the hub) if set; otherwise the active routine's schedule (weekdays = count, cycle = training tiles x 7 / length, every-N = 7 / N, floating = max per week, A/B = average), rounded and kept in 1..7; otherwise 3. Rest days never count against the person.
- [A-P53.2] Streak (engines.md 24): a week is kept at sessions >= target; the week in progress never breaks the streak and counts once kept; a missed week is forgiven by one grace week only if no other grace week lies within 7 weeks and an earlier kept week exists; a grace week is not counted in the number; weeks before the first session are not misses. The current target is applied to every past week. Weeks start on Monday (IST).
- [A-P53.3] Exercise charts: one point per IST day (several sessions on a day merge to the best of each metric); `weight` = heaviest working set, `reps` = most reps in one working set (same reading as the PR board), `e1rm` = best Epley. A missing value is a gap, not a zero. Warm-ups are ignored; unfinished sessions are ignored.
- [A-P53.4] Weekly volume = weight x reps over working sets, 12 IST weeks ending this week, zeros kept, the highest week highlighted (newest on a tie). Consistency grid = 12 weeks, sessions per day.
- [A-P53.5] Plateau hints reuse the engine rule (`isPlateau`: last 4 sessions did not beat the earlier best e1RM by 1 %, needs 5 sessions). Shown on the hub (list) and on the exercise page (one line).
- [A-P53.6] The hub is reached from a Progress button on `/train` and links to Records and History; it does not claim a tab. No new token, icon, dependency or feature key.

### Phase 52 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P52.1] `Ring` and `Gauge` clamp the arc but print the true number (a Ring's centre shows the true percentage, capped at 1,000,000 % so it is always finite). Over 100 % draws a thin `--gold-100` outer arc for the second lap (its length is the part over 100 %, at most one full lap). A negative value reads as 0. `max <= 0` or a non-finite input shows the track, a dash and the "No data yet" summary. A full arc is `--gold-300` (no glow: the CSS filter drew a visible square around the svg).
- [A-P52.2] `HeatGrid`: `to` is also today (outlined) unless a `today` prop is given; days before `from` in the first week and after `to` are not drawn; more than 53 weeks keeps the newest 53; a day listed twice keeps the last value; a bad or negative value counts as 0. Sessions mode bands are 0 / 3 / 5; `mode="volume"` uses quintiles of the non-zero days by share at or below. The "44 px target" is the nearest drawn cell within 22 px of the finger (not an overlay). Most active week = most active days, ties go to the newest. More than 20 weeks scrolls inside its own box. The month label sits on the column where the month of the Monday changes.
- [A-P52.3] `StatTile`: `delta` got two optional fields (`unit`, default `%`, and `label`, for example "vs last week"). The arrow is a CSS triangle (the icon set has no arrows and the spec forbids a new icon in this phase); flat is a short bar. The count-up runs once on mount; a later change of the number shows at once. A string value, a non-finite number, reduced motion, or no `requestAnimationFrame` shows the final text with no count. The big number is hidden from assistive tech and the summary paragraph carries the final text. `href` makes the tile an `<a>`.
- [A-P52.4] `StackedBar`: parts with value <= 0 or not finite are skipped but keep their colour slot; at most 6 parts; `absolute` mode shares one `niceScale` max across rows but draws no axis; `vertical` falls back to horizontal above 8 rows. Segments are buttons (tap again to clear); their readout is a line under the chart (`.sf-ch-stack-info`), not a bubble, so it never covers a bar. A segment shows its percent (percent mode) or value (absolute) inside only when the text fits (about 7 px per character).
- [A-P52.5] `Gauge` marks are tick marks just outside the arc; their values are the legend (shown even for one mark, through a new `legendMin` prop on `ChartFrame`, default 2). Min and max labels sit under the ends.
- [A-P52.6] Summaries for the new charts are built by `summaryFor2` (the Phase 51 `summaryFor` was left as it is). The heat summary says "1 active days" for one day (the spec's template has no plural form).
- [A-P52.7] No new token, icon, dependency, route or feature key. `ProgressRing` and `ProgressBar` were not touched.

### Phase 51 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P51.1] The readout bubble sits at the top of the plot (clamped 18 to 82 % sideways, `width: max-content`, max 70 %) instead of above the point with a flip; it never covers the axis.
- [A-P51.2] No 180 ms tween when data changes after the first reveal (the spec's section 10); a new point simply redraws. Draw-in and bar-grow are pure CSS, once per mount.
- [A-P51.3] `summaryFor` takes a structured input (`SeriesStats` or bar rows) instead of raw data, so the chart builds the stats once; the templates are the spec's, as `chart.sum.*` strings.
- [A-P51.4] A numeric (non-date) x axis spaces the points equally by rank; a date axis is proportional to days (IST `DayKey`s). Mixed series on one chart use the date axis.
- [A-P51.5] Fewer than 2 real points: the chart still draws (a dot with its value shown in the readout), the summary says "Not enough data yet". A chart with no real point shows the empty state with a dashed baseline.
- [A-P51.6] `AreaChart` is a thin wrapper over the same `LineBase` (exported as `AreaOf`); bars are capped at the latest 52; a bar value that is negative or not finite is drawn as 0.
- [A-P51.7] The `unit` prop is appended to every readout row of a chart; a chart that mixes units passes a `format` instead (the preview's two lines are both kg).
- [A-P51.8] The `--chart-1..6`, `--chart-grid` and `--chart-axis` tokens already existed in `tokens.css`; no token was added.

### Phase 48 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P48.1] Size classes: engines.md 5 names large, medium and "small ... etc." Detailed muscles follow the spec's lists by name (quads_lower, rhomboids_teres, upper and mid/lower traps, the three delts, upper and lower chest take their parent's class); the "etc." small class is forearms, calves, soleus, tibialis_anterior, neck, adductors, abductors, rotator_cuff, hip_flexors and serratus_anterior. `full_body`, `cardio` and unknown ids have no clock.
- [A-P48.2] Balance sides (a = push / upper / front, b = pull / lower / back): push = chest group, delts (front, side), triceps, serratus; pull = back group, lats, rhomboids, rear delts, traps, biceps, forearms; upper = push + pull + neck + rotator cuff; lower = quads, hamstrings, glutes, calves group, adductors, abductors; front = chest group, front delts, biceps, abs, obliques, hip flexors, quads, adductors, tibialis; back = back group, rear delts, traps, triceps, lower back, glutes, hamstrings, calves, abductors. A muscle not on an axis is not counted there (abs and lower back are on front/back only).
- [A-P48.3] Balance verdict: the smaller side is a percent of the bigger side (docs/HEATMAP.md wording "Pull is 40% of your push work"); >= 75% is `balanced`; under 6 weighted sets on an axis is `no_data` (the same 6 as the heat floor). The caller passes raw heat, normally the last 28 days.
- [A-P48.4] Neglected = a tracked leaf muscle with no heat in the period (a core id with heat covers its children), never-trained first, then longest since last trained, ties by id; `daysSince` is whole days.
- [A-P48.5] The clock takes `{at (ms UTC), weights}` entries and a `now`; a muscle's time is its latest set at weight >= 0.6, a core id takes the latest of its own and its children's, a future time counts as just trained, and a muscle never trained is `fresh`. `sore` marks do not change the recovery state in this phase (they only demote suggestions, as engines.md 6 says).
- [A-P48.6] One mark per muscle (client_id `mk:<muscle>`); `avoid` wins over `sore` when old rows disagree; a core mark covers its children and an own mark on a child wins over its parent's (`markOf`). `setMuscleMark` also removes older rows for the same muscle with another client_id.

### Phase 45 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P45.1] Anchors: published = squat/bench/deadlift (checked against the abstract record); derived = a fixed ratio of those (overhead press 0.65 and barbell row 0.85 of bench, close-grip bench 0.90 of bench, Romanian deadlift 0.85, good morning 0.40, hip thrust 0.92 of deadlift; female uses the same ratio x the female anchor); everything else = estimate. [A-P45.2] Assisted lifts are not mapped (assistance weight undefined). [A-P45.3] Carries, holds, plyometrics, Olympic lifts, swings, cardio, mobility, bands, suspension, balls and neck work have no ladder. [A-P45.4] One dumbbell/kettlebell = `total` with a low factor; two dumbbells = `per_hand` (x2). [A-P45.5] Groups come from `parent_group` in the detailed taxonomy; neck/full_body/cardio have none. [A-P45.6] Every mapped exercise has its own exact-slug rule; the 13 word rules are a low-confidence net for future exercises and fire on nothing today. [A-P45.7] Tier fractions and experience thresholds are copied unchanged from engines.md 26.2 and 26.6.

### Phase 42 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P42.1] `VITE_SHARE_RELAY_URL` is the Edge Functions base URL (e.g. `https://xyz.supabase.co/functions/v1`); the client appends `/share_create` etc. The anon key is sent as `apikey` and `Authorization: Bearer`.
- [A-P42.2] Even when the link carries a `#secret`, the claim waits for the **Get plan** tap. The claim deletes the row, so an automatic claim on page load (reload, double mount) could burn the share before the person saw it. This also keeps "no network before the user taps".
- [A-P42.3] "Hidden by your gym" is wired (`importPlan` flag, preview line) but the hidden list is empty until the gym link phase supplies it; `setGymRelay()` is the hook for the gym's functions URL (Phase 66).
- [A-P42.4] Settings -> Privacy is a new sub-page at `/settings/privacy` (the `/me` screen is still a placeholder, so it is linked from the Share sheet via "What is shared?"). Buttons: Share plan in the routine's options menu; Receive a plan on the Routines list. Both hidden unless `plan_share` is on and a relay is configured; the `/share` route itself shows "Sharing isn't set up on this app yet." when no relay.
- [A-P42.5] Poll: 3 s, max 60; three failures in a row end as "lost the connection" (offer Cancel / Share again). Delivered = relay says `gone` before the expiry and the person did not cancel.
- [A-P42.6] A received plan is saved by `savePlan`: custom exercises first (new ids and slugs), then the routine inactive, then optionally made active. No dedupe of a plan received twice (it becomes "Name 2").
### Phase 41 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P41.1] The pure modules take randomness (secret, IV), the clock (`createdAt`) and ids (`makeId`) as parameters, so `src/shared/` stays free of clock and RNG. WebCrypto and `CompressionStream` are used as globals.
- [A-P41.2] Envelope custom items refer to their exercise by its own `slug` (the routine's `custom` ref holds an id, which is local and is never sent). On import the ref becomes the new custom `id`. Every custom ref must match an exercise in the envelope, else the envelope is `invalid`.
- [A-P41.3] "Never included": custom exercise `notes` are stripped (personal). A routine item's own `note` stays (it is part of the routine shape in engines.md). `scheduleMeta` (deload weeks, anchor), `archived`, `active`, all ids and any unknown field are never copied; a received envelope has unknown fields dropped by zod.
- [A-P41.4] Receive limits beyond the contract (so a hostile sender cannot flood the app): <= 7 days, <= 40 items per day, <= 60 custom exercises, name 40, note 200, sender name 30, `repMin <= repMax`. A bad `schedule` is dropped (plan still imports); everything else wrong is `invalid` -> shown as "corrupt".
- [A-P41.5] `decryptEnvelope` returns `corrupt` for a wrong secret, a flipped bit, bad gzip, bad JSON or a bad shape (the user-facing failure is the same), `too_new` when `contractMajor` is higher than the caller's, `too_large` above the caps. Gunzip output is capped at 64 KB (zip-bomb guard).
- [A-P41.6] Imported routine is `source: 'mine'`, `active: false`; Phase 42 flips `active` for "Add and make active". Routine name collisions get " 2", " 3" (case-insensitive, kept within 40 characters).
- The Phase 41 fixtures were generated by a throwaway `node:crypto` script (not in the repo), so the vectors are independent of the app code.

### Phase 37 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P37.1] Rules are `contract/engines.md` section 17: `weight` = heaviest set (reps >= 1), `reps` = most reps in one set at any weight, `volume` = biggest SINGLE set weight x reps, `e1rm` = Epley (reps 1-12). Each is beaten alone; ties are not PRs; warm-up and drop sets ignored; a set with no weight only feeds `reps`. This differs from the Phase 36 `engine/pr.ts` (session volume, reps at the same weight), which stays for the overload engine.
- [A-P37.2] First-log rule: no earlier result under the key -> baseline, no PR; the second session compares normally. If the exercise has results under another grip (per-grip PRs on) the first value under the new grip counts, with `previous: null`. A metric with no earlier value but an earlier session (first weighted set after bodyweight sets) also counts, `previous: null`.
- [A-P37.3] "Separate PRs per grip" reads the existing pref (default off). The key is `slug` or `slug#grip:<grip>`, the same as `prKey` (a test checks they match).
- [A-P37.4] The board uses finished sessions only (an end time); skipped items and items with no working set are left out. A session being judged (`sessionPRs`) may still be open and is compared with finished sessions that started before it. Custom exercises are keyed by their slug.
- [A-P37.5] Cards are ordered by the most recent day trained, then key. Each record shows the IST day of the session that set it.
- [A-P37.6] The toast: only the text helper (`prToastText`) and the repo call (`prsForSession`) are built; the logger and the summary do not exist yet, so nothing displays a toast. Route sits under the Me tab as a sub-page with a Back button to `/me`.
- No new dependency, no new icon.

### Phase 36 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P36.1] Written defaults of `PROGRESSIVE_OVERLOAD_RULES.md` are used as they stand; no owner prototype was in the zip. `pr.ts` follows that file ("rep PR = most reps at the same weight within 0.01 kg", needs an earlier result at that weight). `contract/engines.md` section 17 (best weight / best reps at any weight / best volume of one set) differs; Phase 37 builds that board in `src/shared/pr.ts` and may reuse these helpers.
- [A-P36.2] History is oldest first; an entry is one exercise in one session. A session with no working set is dropped. A set is working when its tag is normal or superset and reps >= 1.
- [A-P36.3] "No progress" (for deload and plateau streaks): the session's top working weight is not higher than the one before, and at the same top weight the total reps are not higher. The first session has no predecessor, so a deload needs 4 sessions (5 for Beast) of history for the double rule; the doc's "3 sessions with no progress" is read as 3 sessions each not beating the one before. Skipped when the latest session already reached the top of the range.
- [A-P36.4] Bodyweight (all working weights 0 or null): no weight is suggested (`weightKg` 0, note `bodyweight`); each set below the rep max gets +1 rep.
- [A-P36.5] Linear: the target reps per set are `repMax` (a 5x5 item has repMin = repMax = 5); a session qualifies when every working set reaches it; RPE is not checked. Double: every set reaches `repMax` and no logged RPE is above 9. A missed linear session holds the weight.
- [A-P36.6] Light needs two qualifying sessions in a row at the same top weight; with one it holds (note `hold_wait`). Two steps (Hard RPE <= 7, Beast RPE <= 8) need every set at `repMax + 2` reps with RPE logged. The 10% clamp is read literally: the jump is at most max(10% of last weight, one step), rounded down to whole steps, so Hard at 40 kg with a 2.5 kg step still gets one step. Adds are rounded down to a multiple of the step; a deload goes to the nearest multiple and never above the last weight.
- [A-P36.7] Plateau needs >= 5 sessions with an e1RM; "improved by >= 1%" is exactly 1% counted as progress. Level-up suggestion is not built (needs weeks of plan data; not in this phase's file list).
- No new dependency, no route, no string, no CSS, no UI.

### Phase 35 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P35.1] Inputs per P14.7: skipped exercise, pool (all exercises with the same first primary muscle plus the skipped one's substitutes), variation texts, substitution slugs. \"Same primary muscle\" is the index `primary[0]` (custom exercises: the first primary row); \"pattern\" is the detail `pattern` string ('' never matches, so a custom exercise skips pool 2 and gets pool 4).
- [A-P35.2] Pain with no area picked uses the exercise's own primary muscle rows (detailed ids), as P14.6 says. The picked area is only used for this call; no mark is saved (not in the phase scope).
- [A-P35.3] Chip text: pool 1 = Same exercise; pool 3 = Substitute; pools 2 and 4 = Same muscle; for reasons Pain and Too hard a candidate easier than the skipped one shows Easier instead. The spec listed the chip names, not these rules.
- [A-P35.4] Swap: the original becomes `skipped` with the chosen reason (a `done` original stays `done`); the new item is `todo` at `order + 1` with the same planned sets; a same-exercise variation keeps the setup, another exercise does not. Undo puts the whole session back as it was before the swap (any later change clears the Undo first).
- [A-P35.5] Mark machine busy is a button on the Machine busy reason; it stores the machine on the session (`busyMachines`, local, survives a reload) and hides that machine's exercises from the alternatives for any reason. It does not touch the add sheet.
- [A-P35.6] Skip on any card now opens the sheet (the old one-tap skip is Just skip inside it). Just skip records the reason only when a reason chip was tapped. Swap opens the sheet with reason Other already chosen.
- [A-P35.7] One new fixture file; the expected values are the Phase 14 worked examples for the 5 cases; the fixture embeds only the pools those cases need.
- No new dependency, no new icon, no new route, no new CSS.

### Phase 34 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P34.1] The contract `SessionItem` has no planned sets, so items carry local extras `plan { sets, repMin, repMax, restSec }` (and `supersetGroup`), and the session carries `dayName`. Nothing in `contract/` changed.
- [A-P34.2] "Suggested" day on `/train` = the rotation pointer (`pointer % days`) of the active routine (or the first live one); it does not read the schedule's weekday or recovery logic. Home's Today card still uses the full schedule.
- [A-P34.3] Finishing (a basic S5) always works: no sets logged -> Discard (confirm, deletes) or Keep; some unfinished -> counts by status and Drop them (default) / Keep for next time; the unfinished refs go to prefs `leftover:<dayId>` and are added once to that day's next session. Skipped, later, todo and doing all count as "not done". After finish the app goes to `/train` (summary is Phase 39).
- [A-P34.4] Finishing a day-based session moves the rotation pointer to the day after it; "Start new anyway" ends the old session as it is without moving it. Discard moves nothing.
- [A-P34.5] On the card list open items (todo, doing) come first in order, then skipped, then done (dimmed); Later items are in a second section. Up / Down swap with the neighbouring visible item. Duplicates are allowed; the second and later copies get a number badge.
- [A-P34.6] `Repeat last` copies the newest finished session that had exercises; the routine / day ids are kept.
- [A-P34.7] Only one session is open at a time; `/session/new` with one open offers Resume or Start new anyway; Cancel goes to `/train`.
- No new dependency, no new icon, no `contract/` change.

### Phase 33 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P33.1] Day plan by time box: warm-ups 0 / 1 / 2 (under 30 / 30+ / 45+ min), main lifts 1 / 2 / 3 (under 30 / under 50 / more), isolation 2 / 3 / 4, finisher from 45 min, cooldown from 30 min. Defaults: warm-up 2 x 10-15 rest 30; main 3 x 6-10 rest 120; isolation 3 x 10-15 rest 60; finisher 2 x 10-20 rest 45; cooldown 1 x 8-12 rest 15. Sets shrink toward a minimum before a slot is dropped. Fewer than the plan is allowed when time runs out.
- [A-P33.2] Quick counts: 15 min = 1 main + up to 2 isolation (2-3 exercises), 30 = 2 + up to 3 (4-5) as in engines.md 21; 20 = 3-4, 45 = 5-6, 60 = 6-7 are mine. Quick has no warm-up, finisher or cooldown; the ramp-up note is on the first main only from 30 min. At 15 min the first main is paired with a listed partner when one fits (counts as two). Quick rep time uses the top of the rep range.
- [A-P33.3] Main and isolation picks must have the target muscle as a primary; muscles are used in turn (main i takes muscle i, isolation continues the rotation), skipping to the next muscle when none fits. Warm-up and cooldown only need to touch the muscle. Finisher = category `other` with sub-category conditioning, cardio machine or plyometric, ranked by the normal score.
- [A-P33.4] A split label maps to muscles through the Phase 30 split table ("Full body B" reads as Full body; "Mobility" has no muscles, so nothing is built). The sheet uses the day's target muscles, else the muscles of its name.
- [A-P33.5] Shuffle keeps sets, reps and rest, so the time is unchanged; `tried` is kept on the screen per position and the list starts again when all were seen.
- [A-P33.6] Using a built day replaces the day's items, or (when the day has items) offers Add to the end, which skips exercises already in the day. Nothing is saved until you choose.
- [A-P33.7] Quick workout can only be kept as a new day (named "Quick <n> min") in the active routine, or the first routine when none is active; a routine with 7 days cannot take it.
- [A-P33.8] Superset partners come from the compiled pairs file: antagonist first, then compatible. Only library exercises (not custom ones) have partners. Pairing adds the partner to the day when it is missing, then links the two.
- [A-P33.9] Level offers Any / Beginner and Goal offers Any / Muscle / Strength / Fat loss in the sheet, because no profile preference exists; Quick workout does not use them.
- No new dependency, no new icon, one new route.

### Phase 31 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P31.1] Every change autosaves at once through `putRoutine` (no Save-the-routine button); sheets (item, muscles, rename) save on their own Save and Cancel discards.
- [A-P31.2] The docs gave no number for the soft cap, so: a muscle is "planned two days in a row" when it has >= 4 weighted sets on each of two consecutive days (the last day counts as followed by the first), and the weekly cap is 20 weighted sets. Weighted sets use the engine weights (primary/secondary by emphasis, stabiliser x0.5). Change the two constants in `src/shared/routineEdit.ts` if the trainer says otherwise.
- [A-P31.3] The week card has no Start button yet: there is no session to start before Phase 34. It opens the day editor; Phase 34 adds Start.
- [A-P31.4] "Good for <muscle>" shows up to 4 exercises for each of the day's first 3 target muscles, strongest first (`byMuscle`), not the Muscle Lab's ranked three sections.
- [A-P31.5] Day rename is on the day page; routine rename is in the week page menu. A routine name must be unique (case-insensitive) among live routines. Last day cannot be removed; adding or removing a day changes days per week.
- [A-P31.6] Muscle volume is shown per the dataset's own muscle ids, so a Chest day can list Upper Chest and Lower Chest separately.
- [A-P31.7] Up/Down and Drag are text buttons because the icon set has no arrows or grip and a new icon was out of scope.
- No new dependency, no `contract/` change.

### Phase 30 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P30.1] Setup defaults: 3 days, no weekdays, PPL split, blank name. Choosing a split chip other than Custom opens the preview sheet first (Use this / Back); Custom selects at once.
- [A-P30.2] Flexible creates 3 days and no weekdays (Phase 28 A-P28.2). The stepper is greyed while Flexible is on; tapping Flexible again returns to 3 days.
- [A-P30.3] The first live routine is made active automatically; later ones are not. Deleting the active routine leaves none active (the next Set active picks one).
- [A-P30.4] The list's per-routine menu has Open, Set active, Duplicate, Delete. Rename and Archive (Phase 28 menu) are left out (rename is named in Phase 31; archive has no phase). Delete is a soft delete with a 6 s Undo row.
- [A-P30.5] `docs/FEATURES.md` R-01 is not ticked: it spans Phases 30 and 31 and only the setup half exists.
- Day names are stored as plain English labels ("Push", "Full body A"), like a name the person typed; they are not translated.
- No new dependency, no new icon, no `contract/` change.

### Phase 29 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P29.1] The routine's Days are a rotation with a pointer in every mode; weekdays only choose the dates. So 4 days on 3 weekdays rotate across weeks.
- [A-P29.2] A/B anchor date and deload weeks live in a local `scheduleMeta` on the routine, not in `Schedule`. No contract change now; syncing them would be one later.
- [A-P29.3] Deload factors (sets 60%, loads 90%) are editable in Settings -> Schedule (ranges are mine).
- [A-P29.4] Per-date overrides ("Move here", "Rest") are stored locally on the routine. "Rest" counts as shift and raises no missed sheet.
- Floating readiness score (fresh 3, ready 2, recovering 1, fatigued 0, mean per Day), the ">= 60% fatigued" boundary reading, the "more than 3 missed = Welcome back" rule, the rounding of deload sets (round, min 1) and every-N having no missed-day sheet are my choices.
- Active recovery/mobility category ids are left to Phase 32 to read from the dataset.

### Phase 28 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P28.1] Everything marked [A] in the two Phase 28 sections is my reading; none of it is owner data and no coach has seen it.
- [A-P28.2] Stepper allows 2-7 days; a 1-day routine is only possible via Custom. Flexible creates 3 blank-or-split days.
- [A-P28.3] Session card states, the `doing` status being set by the first logged set, and the "Mark done?" chip (never automatic) are my choices.
- [A-P28.4] Finish: unfinished items are dropped by default; "Keep for next time" stores a "Left over" note on the day.
- [A-P28.5] Only one session is open at a time; starting another finishes the old one as-is.
- Split maps: Full body and Bro (6th, 7th day) are my additions to the existing table; PPL, Upper/Lower and the 5-day Bro are unchanged. Arnold / PHUL / push-pull are left to Phase 29.
- No `contract/` change; `engines.md` sections 1, 8-10 read, not edited.

### Phase 27 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P27.1] Routes: `/library/new` (create) and `/exercise/:slug/edit` (edit) share one page; both are gated by `custom_exercises` (a gated-off route shows the 404 page). `/exercise/my-<slug>` is the Phase 21 page, which already handled custom exercises.
- [A-P27.2] Muscles are stored as the ids the Body Picker reports (detailed ids or a parent such as `chest`; the taxonomy treats a parent as covering its children). The first muscle tapped becomes main/high; later ones helper/medium; the list under the figure sets role (Main / Helper / Stabiliser) and emphasis (High / Medium / Low). At least one main muscle is required.
- [A-P27.3] YouTube link: https only AND a YouTube host (`youtube.com`, `www.`, `m.`, `music.` or `youtu.be`); empty is allowed. The spec said "validated https"; limiting the host is my choice.
- [A-P27.4] Duplicate-name guard compares names ignoring case, accents, punctuation and extra spaces, against all 634 dataset names and your other custom exercises (not the one being edited, not deleted ones).
- [A-P27.5] Editing keeps the slug even when the name changes, so routines and history that name the slug keep working. A new exercise gets `my-<kebab>` (`-2`, `-3` on a clash).
- [A-P27.6] The chosen movement pattern and its bench angle are stored on the custom row as `patternId` + `param` (extra fields; A-P24.4). A pattern id that no longer exists is dropped on save. The exercise page reads them for the figure.
- [A-P27.7] Delete is a soft delete (tombstone). No undo.
- [A-P27.8] Notes are limited to 500 characters (same limit as the personal note); rep range limits come from the Phase 12 validator (whole numbers 1 to 1000, first not above second). Defaults 8 to 12.
- No new dependency, no new icon, no `contract/` change.

### Phase 26 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P26.1] Row shape: one row per exercise in the `favourites` store, client_id `ex:<slug>`, fields `{slug, favourite, hidden, note}`. This replaces the P18.1 reading (favourite = a bare `{slug}` row); a legacy `{slug}` row with none of the three fields is still read as a favourite. The Muscle Lab loader was changed to read the new rows. No new store, no schema change.
- [A-P26.2] Favourite and Never show exclude each other (setting one clears the other). Notes are at most 500 characters, device-only text, saved on leaving the box; over the limit is refused, not cut.
- [A-P26.3] "Hidden" removes an exercise from Library search/counts/chips, the Muscle Lab and suggestion lists, and from Easier, Harder and Substitutes. It is not removed from lookup by slug, so the exercise page and (later) history still open it. The Library has a Hidden exercises view so it can be shown again.
- [A-P26.4] "My go-tos" = favourites that are not hidden, A to Z, shown at the top of the Library exercise tab when the search box is empty. No separate page.
- [A-P26.5] No feature key (none in the catalogue; `contract/` is read-only), so favourites, hide and notes are core screens.
- [A-P26.6] Easier/Harder keeps the Phase 21 family rule; the only change is that hidden exercises are skipped.
- No new dependency, no new icon, no new route, no `contract/` change.

### Phase 25 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P25.1] Kinematics conventions (written out in `API_CONTRACTS.md`, "Built in Phase 25"): side view faces +x with y up; hip, knee, ankle, shoulder, elbow follow `MOVEMENT_PATTERN_NOTES.md`. Segment lengths (male: torso 52, upper arm 30, forearm 33 incl. hand, thigh 43, shin 42, foot 24; female about 6 to 9% shorter) are my own and unreviewed.
- [A-P25.2] Anchors: `feet` fixes the near ankle, `hands`, `hands_feet` and `hang` fix the near wrist, `seat`, `back` and `hips_pad` fix the hip. The `arm_world` shoulder shift uses the pattern's own default torso lean (`bench_deg - 90`, or `torso_lean`, else the start pose's lean).
- [A-P25.3] Setup picker mapping (`paramsFromSetup`): incline uses the setup's `angleDeg` if given, else 30; decline = -15; flat or lying = 0; seated only for vertical press and calf raise; wide/narrow becomes `stance_width` for squat-like patterns (squat, lunge, step-up, leg press, calf raise, jump, lateral lunge, jumping jack, hip thrust, deadlift) and `grip_width` otherwise. `default_param` values the notes do not allow (`stance_width=shoulder`, `seated=no`) are ignored.
- [A-P25.4] Arc text sits in a legend row under the figure (not inside the SVG) so three arcs never overlap labels; the arcs themselves are drawn on the joints. Arc joints read the near (left) limb.
- [A-P25.5] Reduced motion and "static side by side" are the same picture: faded start under solid end in one figure. Animation: the live pose blends start to end with an ease, twice, then rests.
- [A-P25.6] The machine page shows a Movement block (a section with < > buttons), not a swipe tab, to keep the page one scroll; the muscle chips there are the primary muscles without setup weighting.
- [A-P25.7] A cable glyph draws a dashed line from the hand to a pulley dot just beyond where the path starts; other glyphs are small gold shapes at the hand. These are schematic, not drawings of the equipment.
- [A-P25.8] The `movement` file is lazy and optional; if it is missing or malformed the exercise page shows the muscle map and the quiet note. Custom exercises have no pattern yet, so they also show the note until Phase 27.
- No new dependency, no new route, no change to existing generated files except the manifest.

### Phase 24 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P24.1] 54 patterns instead of "about 30": 85% coverage needed the small ones (each covers 1 to 5 exercises). Owner may prune; without the 9 patterns added last (rollout, dead bug, clean, side bend, triceps kickback, lateral lunge, cable crunch, jumping jack, slam: 27 exercises) about 81% are mapped.
- [A-P24.2] All angles, anchors, the `arm_world` rule (free-weight arms keep their direction in the room: shoulder += change in torso_lean) and the param effects are my authoring, not from a source, and unseen by a coach.
- [A-P24.3] Joint conventions: side view shoulder = upper arm versus the shoulder-to-hip line; elbow, hip, knee, ankle are inside angles; `torso_lean` + forward, - back; front view hip = sideways spread and `torso_lean` = sideways lean. Chosen so a pose is whole degrees and the 12 joints suffice.
- [A-P24.4] Added columns beyond engines.md 19: `anchor`, `arm_world`, `default_param`, `twin_start`, `twin_end`, `coach_review` (and `rule_id, kind, match_on, applies_to, confidence` in the rules file). The map keeps the four named columns.
- [A-P24.5] Incline press is `horizontal_press` + `bench_deg=30` (no separate pattern); face pull maps to `rear_fly` (ambiguous).
- [A-P24.6] Left unmapped on purpose: frontal-plane work, wrist/forearm, neck, rolling/stretching/mobility, rotator-cuff and scapular drills, multi-phase lifts, side planks, pikes (reasons in `MOVEMENT_PATTERN_NOTES.md`).
- No new dependency, no code, no new route, no new string in `src/i18n/en.ts` (the `mm.*` keys are listed in `docs/MOVEMENT_MAP.md` for Phase 25).

### Phase 23 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P23.1] Two or more chips are computed at runtime with the compiler's own simplified rules (A59), so a one-chip result equals the compiled table. The full `applySetup` (Phase 13) is not used here.
- [A-P23.2] The phase's routes `/machines` and `/machines/:slug/lab` were added; the Phase 21 page `/machine/<slug>` stays and gets a "Machine Lab" link (not a real tab).
- [A-P23.3] Heat band = `ceil(reach * 5)`; text-twin strength words: >= 0.8 strong, >= 0.5 moderate, >= 0.3 light, else faint (my choice, editable).
- [A-P23.4] Chip groups come from the compiled table (bench and handle from the companions, position from the filters the compiler built); one value per axis, tapping the chosen chip clears it. Chips for inferred gear are shown too; if nothing in the data uses them the empty state says so.
- [A-P23.5] With no chip on, each listed exercise shows the single bench/handle/position value that raises its weight most; with chips on it shows the chosen chips. The exercise link carries only the first chosen chip.
- [A-P23.6] The machine finder on `/machines` reuses the Phase 18 `machineFinder` (top 5, skips machines under maintenance or out of order).
- No new dependency, no new icon, `contract/` changed (fixtures only).

### Phase 22 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P22.1] Only the exercise page exists as a host today. Routine items, the set logger and swap suggestions do not exist yet, so `SetupPicker` and `itemFields` are ready and tested but mounted only on the exercise page; those phases mount them.
- [A-P22.2] "Heatmap/recovery use the chosen setup" = the exercise page's muscle effect and mini body use `setupWeights`; the heatmap and recovery screens read the same function when they are built. Muscles a setup adds that the exercise does not list show as secondary on the mini body.
- [A-P22.3] The "separate PRs per grip" Settings toggle: Settings screens are later, so only the pref (`getSeparatePrPerGrip`/`setSeparatePrPerGrip`, default off) and `prKey` exist.
- [A-P22.4] Choosing a chosen chip clears it (back to the default); there is no explicit "Default" chip, a "Back to the usual setup" button shows when anything is chosen.
- [A-P22.5] Custom exercises offer no setup axes yet (rule 3 of `docs/SETUP_OPTIONS.md` belongs to the custom-exercise phase).
- [A-P22.6] The remembered setup is per exercise and is read when the page opens; a `?setup=` url overrides only the axis it names and is then remembered like any choice.

### Phase 21 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P21.1] Section order follows `docs/EXERCISE_PAGE.md` with the form cue card first (when `form_cues` is on and not dismissed) and the YouTube button after cautions. The page is long on purpose (no expanding cards, `UI` rule); say so if you want a different order.
- [A-P21.2] The dataset has no separate "common mistake" field, so the card's mistake slide is the exercise's `injury_cautions` text; no mistake slide when there is none. Cues are the first 3 of the dataset cues.
- [A-P21.3] Easier / Harder = nearest lower / higher difficulty among exercises with the same category and sub-category that share a primary muscle. Hidden when none exists. Custom exercises have none.
- [A-P21.4] "Evidence is mixed" is shown on a muscle whose dataset note contains `contested`, `unconfirmed` or `mixed` (72 notes match). The caution text itself is never parsed.
- [A-P21.5] Tapping the chosen variation again clears it. The choice is stored per exercise in device prefs (`exercise-variation:<slug>`), not yet attached to logged sets (the logger is a later phase; Phase 22 reads it).
- [A-P21.6] Machine page "back" goes to `/library?tab=machines`; exercise page "back" goes to `/library`.
- [A-P21.7] A muscle that appears under two roles in the dataset (for example lats primary and secondary) is listed once, under its strongest role.

### Phase 20 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P20.1] Alias kinds all expand the query (one pass, longest phrase first); documents also carry alias tokens at weight 0.9 so a half-typed alias still matches. A one-letter alias part such as `e z bar` is matched before one-letter words are dropped; a word repeated right after an expansion (`ez` -> `ez bar`, then `bar`) is dropped.
- [A-P20.2] Setup words: read from the words as typed and from the expanded words; `grip`, `handle`, `width`, `stance`, `position` beside a setup word are optional; a setup word is required in the first pass and becomes a bonus only if the first pass finds nothing. `suggestedSetup` needs the top exercise to offer the value; for `rope pushdown` the top exercise (cable triceps pushdown) does not list a rope handle in the data, so no chip is shown (data limit, not a bug).
- [A-P20.3] `didYouMean` = each unknown word (not a known word, not a digit, not the start of a known word) replaced by the closest known word within the edit allowance (ties: used in more names/aliases, then A to Z); shown only when the corrected query finds something under the same filters.
- [A-P20.4] The Library's chip opens `/exercise/<slug>?setup=<axis>:<value>`; the exercise page (Phase 21/22) reads it. Machines tab, Muscle Lab and other screens keep their Phase 11/19 search for now.
- [A-P20.5] Machine documents appear in filtered searches only when no filter is set; with a filter they are dropped (filters are exercise filters). Empty query = browse via the Phase 11 search (A to Z).
- [A-P20.6] Speed budget test = worst of the 29 fixture queries after one warm-up, on the build machine, limit 50 ms; it says nothing about a phone.
- 2026-11-19 | Phase 19 DONE (E, no version bump). Added the Library: `src/shared/equipment.ts`, `src/pages/library/{libraryState.ts,LibraryPage.tsx}`, `src/data/repos/library.ts`, `tests/pages/library.test.ts`; `/library` is now a real screen (registry entry points to it, still core, no flag); `lib.*` strings in `en.ts`; Library styles appended to `body.css`. Features: exercises | machines tabs, search as you type (URL updated by replace), category chips with counts and sub-category chips under a chosen category, filters sheet (kind, difficulty, machine, beginner friendly, one side at a time), result counts, machine cards with photo thumbs and a category chip row, recent searches kept on the device (saved on blur or Enter, removable), equipment mode chips (feature `equipment_mode`). Tests: typecheck (first call failed only because `node_modules` was missing; after `npm install --legacy-peer-deps` it was run seven more times, each stopping at the next `Input` prop typing error (`type`, `enterKeyHint`, `autoComplete`, `placeholder`, `value`), the last run clean: eight calls in all, not one; I should have read `InputProps` first), `tests/pages/library.test.ts` 15 pass (first run: 1 failure, machine text search matched "lat" inside "flat bench"; fixed in `filterMachines` to match from the start of a word, then re-run of that file only). One 360 px browser check passed (see table row). NOT run: build, guard test, other suites. Gaps: see Standing gaps (Phase 19 line).

### Phase 19 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P19.1] The whole filter state lives in the URL query (`?tab&q&cat&sub&kind&diff&beginner&uni&machine&mcat`), so a library view can be linked, reloaded and used with the back button; typing and chips replace the history entry. Equipment preset and recent searches are device prefs, not URL.
- [A-P19.2] `contract/dataset/equipment_presets.csv` does not exist (DATASET_GUIDE says Phase 24 authors it), so the preset table is a constant in `src/shared/equipment.ts`: `gym` = everything; `bodyweight` = exercises with no machine + yoga mat; `dumbbells` = + dumbbells and rack; `bands` = + resistance tubes; `home` = bodyweight + dumbbells + bands + the four plain benches (adjustable, flat, utility, flat/incline combo). Exercises with no machine are always allowed. Kettlebells, medicine ball, stability balls, suspension trainer, battle rope and the Olympic bar are in no preset except `gym`. Default preset is `gym`. Change only on the owner's word.
- [A-P19.3] Category chips show counts and are computed after the other filters and the preset (so the numbers match what a tap would show); sub-category chips appear only under a chosen category and only when it has 2+ types. A "No machine" option is not offered in the library machine filter (the Muscle Lab has one).
- [A-P19.4] The machine search matches each typed word from the start of a word in name, brand and category (so "lat" finds Lat Pulldown, not Flat Bench). Exercise text search is the Phase 11 search unchanged.
- [A-P19.5] Lists show 30 rows with a "Show 30 more" button (same idea as Phase 18). Machines are shown A to Z with all 55 on one page.
- [A-P19.6] Recent searches: 2+ characters, max 8, newest first, case and spacing ignored, saved when the search box loses focus or Enter is pressed.
- No new dependency, no new icon, no `contract/` change.

### Phase 18 assumptions and decisions
- [A-P18.1] Stored row shapes the lab reads (API_CONTRACTS.md did not name them): favourite row `{slug}`, mark row `{muscle, kind: 'avoid'|'sore'}`; routines per engines.md (`active`, `days[].items[].ref {kind:'lib', slug}`). A row that does not fit is ignored. Change only on the owner's word.

- [A-P18.4] "Muscles worked" for a **machine** uses the reach table: weight 1 = primary, anything lower = secondary (the machine file has no roles); the screen says it is an estimate.
- [A-P18.5] Figure sex defaults to male and is a local toggle (no profile setting exists yet). `/body` is still a core route (feature `null`); inside it, switching `muscle_lab` off shows an "off" state. `/muscle/<id>` is gated by `muscle_lab` by the shell. Why chips follow `why_chips`; the machine finder follows `machine_finder`.

### Phase 17 assumptions and decisions
- Figures are extracted once by `scripts/extract-body-figures.mjs` and committed (generated files, like `src/dataset/generated`). Generated figure files sit under `src/` so the guard test scans them: it passes (no hex, no truncation CSS).
- Muscle names come from `t('muscle.<id>')` (34 new strings), not from the dataset, so the component is synchronous apart from loading its figure.
- Parent ids in `value`, `heat`, `recovery`, `marks` and `emphasis` apply to their drawn children; a child's own entry wins (`lookup`).
- Long-press selects the parent group id (e.g. `chest`); in `pick-one` it replaces the selection, in `pick-many` it toggles.
- Preview section uses a shortened "Rest" label because "Recovery" wrapped mid-word at 320 px.

### Phase 15 assumptions and decisions
- [A84] Reason selection rules and thresholds (see `API_CONTRACTS.md` P15 notes): priority order, 2 max, rested >= 3 days, < 3 sets, 50% of the weekly average with >= 3 trained muscles.
- [A85] The engine takes `Candidate` rows from the caller (no dataset import). A custom exercise has `tracks: 0`, so it never gets the track bonus and never matches a track filter. Custom `rows` use the compiled letters (p/s/t/m, h/m/l); long names are normalised.
- [A86] `showAll` keeps exercises on a `maintenance`/`out_of_order` machine, flags them `unavailable` and `muscleExerciseList` returns them in a fourth list `unavailable` (not in Suggested/Built/More). Without `showAll` they are dropped.
- [A87] `injurySafe` filter drops an exercise that meets an `avoid` OR a `sore` mark. Candidates for a muscle = any non-mobility row (stabilisers included, scoring 0 for the term), same as `byMuscle`.

### Phase 14 assumptions and decisions
- [A79] Emphasis terms: `primaryEmphasis`/`secondaryEmphasis` are the highest factor among role-p / role-s rows for the target (expanded to detailed muscles); stabiliser and mobility give 0.
- [A80] Slot is an input (`main`/`isolation`/`none`); the muscle list uses `none` (no compound bonus), alternatives use the skipped item's category.
- [A81] Jitter = FNV-1a 32 over IST day key + `|` + slug, scaled to [-0.5, 0.5], 4 dp. Surprise-me hashes `dayKey|surprise|n` over the slug-sorted pool.
- [A82] Alternatives: pools are appended in order (each sorted by adjusted score); pool order beats a global score sort (FLEXIBLE_SESSION.md wording). "Same primary muscle" = first index `primary` id. Reason adjustments: pain/too_hard +0.5 per difficulty step easier, no_time +1 for isolation.
- [A83] `Session.clientId` = `id`; "Just skip" = `skipped` with no `skipReason`; the replacement for a swapped item is a new item at `order + 1`; sets/rep/rest limits 1-20 / 1-1000 / 0-3600.

### Phase 13 assumptions and decisions
- [A74] Stacking order is fixed: grip, width, handle, position, arms, bench; within an axis value the exercise's pattern rows then the `any` rows. Multipliers are applied uncapped and the 1.0 cap happens once at the end (so lats 1 x1.25 x1.25 = 1.5625 -> 1). An absent `more` muscle starts at 0.3 and later `more` rows multiply it; an absent `less` muscle stays absent. Weights that no row touched are returned exactly as given.
- [A75] "Exact variation row wins" is per muscle: any muscle the variation's shift rows name is skipped by the setup shift (`skip` set), whatever the direction.
- [A76] The Phase 12 `exerciseWeights(rows, variation?, shiftTable?)` kept its positional arguments; the setup is an optional 4th argument `{setup, pattern, table}`, so the 12 Phase 12 tests needed no edit. The pattern comes from `setup_options.patterns[slug]` (custom exercises have none, so only `any` rows apply).
- [A77] `angleDeg`, and axis values the shift table has no rows for, change no weights.
- [A78] "Heat/recovery callers take an optional setup" is met only as far as `exerciseWeights`; the heat and recovery engines are Phases 43 and 48.

### Phase 12 assumptions and decisions
- [A69] Validators are plain TypeScript returning `{ok, value}` or `{ok:false, error: code}`; screens map `code` to `t('validation.<code>')`. zod is not installed yet (Phase 13 adds the `Setup` zod schema). Limits: weight 0-1000 kg (2 dp), reps 0-1000 whole, seconds 0-86,400, RPE 1-10 in half steps, names 1-60 characters (trimmed, inner spaces collapsed).
- [A70] IST is a fixed UTC+5:30 (India has no daylight saving), so the gym day is computed with plain arithmetic and no Intl/timezone database. Day keys are `YYYY-MM-DD` strings in IST; weekdays are 0 = Monday (engines.md).
- [A71] `src/shared/` receives data as arguments (the taxonomy is built from the muscle list the caller passes); it imports nothing from `src/dataset`. `rollUpMax` is the only roll-up (max, per engines.md section 4).
- [A72] `applyVariation` treats a muscle that is not in the weights (including a mobility-only muscle, weight 0) as absent: `more` adds it at 0.3, `less` leaves it absent. Shift direction letters are `m` = more, `l` = less (also `more`/`less`). Repeated shifts on one muscle apply in order. Weights are tidied to 6 decimals to hide float noise.
- [A73] `normalizeText` stays in `src/dataset/search.ts` (A64); not moved, nothing needed it in `src/shared/`. `customSlug` caps the kebab part at 60 characters before the `my-` prefix.

### Phase 11 assumptions and decisions
- [A64] `normalizeText`/`tokenize` live in `src/dataset/search.ts` (identical to the compiler's `normText`), not `src/shared/`, because `src/shared/` does not exist until Phase 12. Phase 12 may move them; nothing else depends on the location.
- [A65] Custom exercises are passed INTO the query functions (`opts.custom`) by `src/data/repos/exercises.ts`, so `src/dataset/` never imports the local store. A custom row with a dataset slug, a duplicate slug, or no slug/name is ignored (dataset wins). A custom exercise's index row has `category: 'custom'`, `chunk: -1`, difficulty 2, no tracks; its map rows come from the user's own role/emphasis (`primary`->p, `secondary`->s, `stabiliser`->t; high/medium/low -> h/m/l).
- [A66] Search follows engines.md 14 scoring (exact 1.0, prefix 0.85, fuzzy 0.6, field weights name 1.0 / machine 0.6 / muscle 0.5 / category 0.3, +0.3 in-order, -0.05 per untouched name token; ties shorter name then slug) over name, machine name, primary muscle names, category. Score per token = match kind x best field weight. Filters: machine, category, kind, difficulty (exact), beginner, track, and `muscle` (a core id such as `chest` also matches its detailed children). Empty query = browse A to Z.
- [A67] `byMuscle` weight = emphasis factor (h 1, m 0.6, l 0.3), stabiliser x0.5, mobility excluded (engines.md section 2); an exercise's weight is its highest matching row. Not a replacement for Phase 12's `muscleWeight`.
- [A68] The loader returns Phase 10 files (`setup_options`, `machine_reach`, `search.index`) as untyped `unknown | null`; Phases 13, 20 and 23 add their own types when they read them.

### Phase 10 assumptions and decisions
- [A59] Machine reach per muscle = the highest section-2 weight of that muscle over the machine's exercises (role factor x emphasis factor; stabiliser x0.5, mobility 0), capped at 1; the `+ 0.5 * mean secondary` term in engines.md 15 is not applied because it has no per-muscle meaning. `count` = exercises with weight >= 0.3. Checked against the gap list (see log). engines.md is not changed (contract untouched); Phase 23 may revisit.
- [A60] An alias loop is a cycle through aliases with different canonicals. `rhomboid / rhomboids / teres -> "rhomboids teres"` is a stable one-pass fixed point, not a loop. Duplicate aliases, an alias equal to its canonical and unknown kinds are errors.
- [A61] The exercise's option list is the union of tags over all its variation rows; per-row tags are not stored (the variation text stays the identity). Review file location is `review/untagged_variations.csv` at the project root; it lists tagged-by-ambiguous-rule rows and untagged grip/handle-word rows, not every untagged row (3,339 of 4,752 are plain tempo/load text).
- [A62] Search documents: exercise `machine` field = machine name, machine docs' `machine` field = brand; `muscle` = core primary names plus detailed names of primary/secondary map rows; `setup` = the exercise's offered option values (underscores as spaces); `alias` = aliases of kind exercise/machine whose canonical equals the document name. `setupTerms` is the bare vocabulary (ambiguous words such as "incline" list both axes); Phase 20 decides.
- [A63] Size budgets added: setup_options <= 30 KB gz, machine_reach <= 20 KB gz, search.index <= 250 KB gz; none of them counts toward the 60 KB precached total (Phase 11 decides what is precached).

### Phase 9 assumptions and decisions
- [A55] Source CSVs mix CRLF and LF line endings (about 1,100 of 2,750 lines of `exercise_muscle_map.csv` are CRLF) and papaparse then reports false quote errors. The compiler normalises `\r\n?` to `\n` before parsing; CSVs untouched.
- [A56] The compiler also fails on: blank variation text, duplicate muscle in one exercise's map, duplicate exercise name, difficulty not 1-3, unilateral/beginner not yes/no, only one of rep_min/rep_max set, a size budget exceeded (section 8). The guide-count check can be switched off in `compile()` only for test fixtures.
- [A57] Choices where section 3 was silent: `cues`, machine `cues` split on `;` into arrays; `muscles.json` `regions` = the taxonomy `map_regions` text; `units[].muscle` = `detailed_muscle_id`; `supersets[].muscles` split on `/`; `media` entries ordered by `order` then thumb before detail; manifest `files` lists every logical file except the detail chunks, which are in `detailChunks`; manifest `version` hashes the sorted `logical:hash8` lines of all data files (manifest excluded). Superset muscle ids are not checked against the taxonomy.
- [A58] Machine photos are checked on disk under `public/machines/<slug>/<file>` (all 128 exist).

### Phase 8 assumptions and decisions
- [A48] `reverse_fly` is an eighth exercise pattern (beyond pulldown, row, press, fly, curl, pushdown, raise) because engines.md 13b's own example (reverse pec deck + neutral -> rear delts more, traps less) would otherwise hit chest flyes. `any` applies to every exercise.
- [A49] There is no pattern column in the data, so a pattern is picked from the exercise name by keyword (table in `DATASET_GUIDE.md`), first match wins; covers 193 of 634 exercises. Phase 10/13 implement that table; Phase 24's movement patterns may replace it.
- [A50] "Machine modes" live in `machine_companions.csv` as `kind=mode` rows (separate-exercise modes carry `exercise_slug`; setup modes use `axis:value`), not in a separate file.
- [A51] Columns beyond the phase's minimum were added so the compiler needs no hidden knowledge: `excludes`, `confidence`, `note` (rules); `physio_review`, `confidence`, `reason` (shifts); `evidence`, `label` (companions); `kind` (aliases).
- [A52] Vocabulary folding in the rules: semi-neutral -> neutral; medium / hip width / conventional -> shoulder; sumo -> wide; stance width uses the `width` axis; double overhand -> pronated. `strap` = cable strap handle only; lifting straps stay untagged. Untagged because the vocabulary has no value: hook, snatch, clean grips, bent-over, feet elevated, deficit.
- [A53] No shift rows for squat, deadlift, lunge, leg press, calf work: outside the seven named patterns; listed under Awaiting from owner.
- [A54] `contract/CHECKSUMS.txt` was regenerated in this zip as CONTRACT.md requires; the owner still copies the folder to the Admin zip. `contract/VERSION` stays `1.0.0-draft`.

### Phase 7 assumptions and decisions
- [A43] Detail chunks are also warmed once when idle after first load (about 57 KB gz total), in addition to "cached on first view" from PHASES.md, so exercise pages work offline for users who never opened them. Photos are not warmed.
- [A44] The index carries extra small columns beyond PHASES.md's list (`unilateral`, `beginner`, `tracks` bitmask, `warmup`, `chunk`) so library filters need no detail chunk. "Injury-safe" is computed from avoid marks, not stored (all 634 rows have a caution text).
- [A45] The 42-exercise fallback is expanded at compile time (same result as engines.md section 2); Phase 12 keeps the runtime rule for custom exercises and tests that both agree.
- [A46] Generated files are committed and named `<logical>.<hash8>.json`; Vite chunk names `ds-detail-*` / `ds-core-*` (Phase 11) let the SW separate them. Muscle-map free-text `note` lives in the detail chunk, not in `muscle_map`.
- [A47] Phase 11 search runs on the index + muscle names only; aliases/setup words join later via the optional `search.index.json`.

### Phase 6 assumptions and decisions
- [A37] Function names follow PHASES.md (`exportAll/importAll`); `API_CONTRACTS.md` previously said `exportBackup/importBackup` and was updated.
- [A38] `remove()` is a soft delete (tombstone with `deleted_at`) so sync and backup carry deletions; there is no hard purge yet.
- [A39] The outbox switch is a module flag (`setOutboxEnabled`), off by default; Phase 66/67 turn it on when a gym link is active. `prefs` (local only per SYNC_SPEC) and `photos` never queue. Backup import also queues outbox rows when connected.
- [A40] Photos are stored as data URLs in a `data` string (not Blobs) so backup JSON can carry them without conversion.
- [A41] Import keeps device rows that are not in the file (merge, never wipe); an incoming row replaces a device row only if strictly newer.
- [A42] `updated_at` / `deleted_at` are ms since epoch (UTC) from an injectable clock (`setClock`); `client_id` is `crypto.randomUUID()` with a fallback.
