Installed vite-plugin-pwa 2.0.0, workbox-build/window/precaching/routing/strategies 7.4.1, qrcode-generator 2.0.4. Tests run: typecheck (twice), `tests/pwa/installGate.test.ts` (9 pass), `tests/pwa/bypass.test.ts` (skipped, no `dist/`), plus a Playwright check of the gate on the Vite dev server. NOT run: build, guard test, real devices. Gaps above.

### Phase 5 assumptions and decisions
- [A32] The service worker is registered from the browser tab too (before the gate decision), because Android Chrome only offers install with an active worker. It only serves the app's own files.
- [A33] `workbox-strategies@7.4.1` added (not in the original list) for `CacheFirst`; no `workbox-expiration`, so cached photos are never evicted yet.
- [A34] Update flow: a waiting worker shows `UpdateToast` (own component, since the Phase 3 toast has no action button); Reload sends SKIP_WAITING and the page reloads when the new worker takes control. `version.json` is compared to the compiled-in build id on open and on becoming visible.
- [A35] Desktop = no touch and width >= 768 px; unknown touch devices get the generic Android-style steps.
- [A36] Manifest `theme_color`/`background_color` copy `--bg-base` as a literal (a manifest cannot read CSS); `vite.config.ts` is not scanned by the guard.
- 2026-11-19 | Phase 4 DONE (no version bump). Added `src/app/{routes,flags,Shell,lazyPage}`, `src/pages/{Placeholder,NotFound,dev/DesignPreview}`, `src/i18n/{en,index}.ts`, `src/ui/styles/shell.css`; `main.tsx` now renders the shell; 4 tab icons added via `build-icons`. Installed `wouter-preact@3.13.0` (A26). Tests run: typecheck once (clean); `tests/app` (routes 6 + flags 4 = 10 pass); one Playwright check on `vite` dev server at 360 px: Today, tab click to Library (URL, active tab, `aria-current`), `/exercise/<slug>` placeholder, 404, design preview (toast and sheet open); no horizontal overflow, no targets under 44 px, no console errors. The check found a real bug: `animation ... both` on `.sf-page` kept a stacking context so sheets sat under the tab bar; fixed with `backwards`, rechecked. NOT run: build, guard test, any other width, real devices. Gaps above.

### Phase 4 assumptions and decisions
- [A26] Used `wouter-preact@3.13.0` (the native Preact build, same version) instead of `wouter`, so no react-compat alias is needed in Vite or Vitest. `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md` updated.
- [A27] Core screens (tabs, 404, exercise, machine, dev preview) have `feature: null` (never switched off) because the catalogue has no key for them; only `/routine/:id` (routine_builder) and `/muscle/:id` (muscle_lab) are gated. A gated-off route shows the 404 page.
- [A28] Tab order and names follow PHASES.md (Today, Body, Train, Library, Me), not the older list in `docs/design/UI_RULES.md` (Home, Workout, Progress, Level, Me). Tab icons by eye: house, person-simple, barbell, books, user-circle.
- [A29] Registry lives at `src/app/routes.ts` as the phase says (PROJECT_STRUCTURE's planned layout said `src/routes.ts`; note added there).
- [A30] Flags in Phase 4 have no user switches (prefs store is Phase 6, switches UI is Phase 62); only standalone defaults and the policy hook point.
- [A31] Tab bar height 64 px plus safe-area; `--toast-offset` is set to it inside `.sf-shell`. Page transition = 180 ms fade + 8 px rise, disabled by the global reduced-motion rule.

### Phase 3 assumptions and decisions
- [A20] Component styling is plain CSS classes (`.sf-*` in `components.css`), not Tailwind utilities, so it does not depend on Tailwind's content scan; Tailwind stays available for layout utilities in pages. `vitest.config.ts` include widened to `.test.tsx`.
- [A21] Default accessible labels (Back, Close, Increase...) live in `src/ui/components/labels.ts` as English constants and every one is overridable by prop; Phase 4 (i18n) swaps them for `t('key')`.
- [A22] Router does not exist yet, so `SubPageHeader` takes `onBack`; Phase 4 wires it to history/route.
- [A23] Components use Preact's `class` prop (not `className`). Chip shows a check icon when selected (selection is never colour alone).
- [A24] Toast store is module-level (any code can call `showToast`); max 3 visible, default 3.5 s, `durationMs: 0` = sticky.
- [A25] The 80 px drag threshold for sheet dismiss and the 40 ms stagger step are by-eye defaults.

### Phase 1 assumptions and decisions
- [A10] Phase-1 packages = vite, @preact/preset-vite, preact, typescript, tailwindcss, postcss, autoprefixer, vitest, @types/node. `happy-dom` (component tests) and `fake-indexeddb` (Phase 6) left for the phase that first needs them.
- [A11] `legacy-peer-deps=true` in `.npmrc` because plain install of vitest 5.1.0 crashes npm 10.9.9. Keeps the lockfile and the GitHub `npm ci` consistent. Revisit if npm is upgraded or vitest 5.1.1 installs cleanly (latest tag is 5.1.1; not tried, pin unchanged).
- [A12] Config file is `tailwind.config.js` (as PHASES.md Phase 1 says); PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md listed `.ts`, left as is.
- [A13] Placeholder page text is a hard-coded English string; Phase 4 moves strings to `src/i18n/en.ts`.

### Phase 2 assumptions and decisions
- [A14] DESIGN_TOKENS.md has two tier palettes. `--tier-*` (wood...olympian, the 2026-11-19 muscle-badge set) took the `tier` name; the older general set (bronze, silver, gold, platinum, diamond, shield elite) is `--rank-*`. Values unchanged from the spec.
- [A15] Values the spec did not give, chosen by eye (editable): loss ramp `--loss-1..5` (steel family: #26343B, #4A6674, #7FA6B8, #A9C6D3, #D3E3EA), `--ease-out` cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1), spacing `--sp-7..10` = 32/40/48/64 px, `--touch-min` 44 px, `--mark-avoid-stripe` ivory.
- [A16] Display face = Barlow Condensed (condensed, OFL) over a serif, for readable big numerals; weights 500/600/700. UI face = Inter variable.
- [A17] Tailwind colour keys mirror token names except text colours: `ink`, `ink-2`, `ink-muted`, `ink-on-gold` (avoids `text-text`). `theme.colors`, spacing, radii, font sizes are replaced by token-backed sets, so default Tailwind classes like `p-7`/`text-4xl`/`bg-purple-500` do not exist.
- [A18] Fonts are vendored copies in `src/ui/fonts/` (packages are dev deps only as the source), so builds do not depend on the packages' CSS or load unused subsets.
- [A19] `src/main.tsx` now imports `index.css`; the placeholder page is therefore the only thing it styles.
